    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;d (F-lag,N,3) displacements, sh (F-lag,N) start shell (-1 excluded; K-th extra column = ALL Li).
    Returns per-block sums NUM (nb,K+1) = sum |dr|^2, DEN (nb,K+1) = sum |sum dr|^2, CNT (nb,K+1) = Li-origins, ORG (nb,) origins.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    Fo = d.shape[0]
    d2 = np.einsum(&amp;quot;fni,fni-&amp;gt;fn&amp;quot;, d, d)
    blk = io.block_id(np.arange(Fo), Fo, nblocks)
    NUM = np.zeros((nblocks, K + 1))
    DEN = np.zeros((nblocks, K + 1))
    CNT = np.zeros((nblocks, K + 1))
    for k in range(K + 1):
        m = (sh == k) if k &amp;lt; K else np.ones(sh.shape, bool)
        s = np.einsum(&amp;quot;fni,fn-&amp;gt;fi&amp;quot;, d, m.astype(float))
        num_o = (d2 * m).sum(axis=1)
        den_o = np.einsum(&amp;quot;fi,fi-&amp;gt;f&amp;quot;, s, s)
        cnt_o = m.sum(axis=1).astype(float)
        NUM[:, k] = np.bincount(blk, weights=num_o, minlength=nblocks)
        DEN[:, k] = np.bincount(blk, weights=den_o, minlength=nblocks)
        CNT[:, k] = np.bincount(blk, weights=cnt_o, minlength=nblocks)
    ORG = np.bincount(blk, minlength=nblocks).astype(float)
    return NUM, DEN, CNT, ORG


def plot(rows, lags, names, path, min_n):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    labs = list(dict.fromkeys(r[&amp;quot;dopant&amp;quot;] for r in rows))
    K = len(names)
    cols = [plt.cm.viridis(k / max(K - 2, 1)) for k in range(K - 1)] + [&amp;quot;k&amp;quot;]
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(len(labs), len(lags), figsize=(4.4 * len(lags), 3.3 * len(labs) + 1.2), dpi=150, squeeze=False,
                            sharex=True, sharey=True)
    for ri, lab in enumerate(labs):
        for ci, tau in enumerate(lags):
            a = axs[ri][ci]
            for k in range(K):
                sel = sorted([r for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;dopant&amp;quot;] == lab and r[&amp;quot;shell&amp;quot;] == names[k] and abs(r[&amp;quot;tau_ps&amp;quot;] - tau) &amp;lt; 0.06],
                             key=lambda r: r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;])
                if not sel:
                    continue
                a.errorbar([r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in sel], [r[&amp;quot;H_R&amp;quot;] for r in sel], [r[&amp;quot;H_R_se&amp;quot;] for r in sel], marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, ms=4, capsize=2,
                           color=cols[k], ls=&amp;quot;--&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;-&amp;quot;)
                lo = [r for r in sel if r[&amp;quot;n_Li_mean&amp;quot;] &amp;lt; min_n]
                if lo:
                    a.plot([r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in lo], [r[&amp;quot;H_R&amp;quot;] for r in lo], ls=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, ms=5, mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, mec=cols[k], zorder=5)
            allr = sorted([r for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;dopant&amp;quot;] == lab and r[&amp;quot;shell&amp;quot;] == &amp;quot;all Li&amp;quot; and abs(r[&amp;quot;tau_ps&amp;quot;] - tau) &amp;lt; 0.06],
                          key=lambda r: r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;])
            if allr:
                a.plot([r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in allr], [r[&amp;quot;H_R&amp;quot;] for r in allr], color=&amp;quot;C3&amp;quot;, lw=1.2, ls=&amp;quot;:&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;x&amp;quot;, ms=4)
            a.axhline(1, color=&amp;quot;0.5&amp;quot;, lw=0.8)
            a.set_title(f&amp;quot;{lab},  tau = {tau:g} ps&amp;quot;, fontsize=10)
            if ri == len(labs) - 1:
                a.set_xlabel(&amp;quot;T (K)&amp;quot;)
            if ci == 0:
                a.set_ylabel(&amp;quot;Haven ratio  MSD_tr / MSD_col  (Li of the shell)&amp;quot;, fontsize=8)
    h = []
    for k in range(K):
        n = np.nanmean([r[&amp;quot;n_Li_mean&amp;quot;] for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;shell&amp;quot;] == names[k]])
        h.append(Line2D([], [], color=cols[k], marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, lw=2, ls=&amp;quot;--&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;-&amp;quot;,
                        label=f&amp;quot;Li starting {names[k]} from dopant (mean {n:.1f} Li)&amp;quot; + (&amp;quot; (far)&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;)))
    h.append(Line2D([], [], color=&amp;quot;C3&amp;quot;, ls=&amp;quot;:&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;x&amp;quot;, label=&amp;quot;all Li together (same tau)&amp;quot;))
    h.append(Line2D([], [], color=&amp;quot;0.4&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, ls=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;, label=f&amp;quot;open symbol: &amp;lt; {min_n:g} Li in the shell on average (H_R -&amp;gt; 1 by construction)&amp;quot;))
    fig.legend(handles=h, loc=&amp;quot;lower center&amp;quot;, ncol=2, fontsize=8, frameon=False)
    fig.suptitle(&amp;quot;Intra-shell Haven ratio by distance to the dopant (1 = Li move independently, &amp;lt; 1 = correlated; error bars = block jackknife)&amp;quot;,
                 fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.13, 1, 0.95))
    fig.savefig(path)
    plt.close(fig)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;target&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;folder with the dumps + element_list (or a settings .yaml)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--defaults&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--T&amp;quot;, type=int, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--tmin&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--outdir&amp;quot;, default=&amp;quot;haven09&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--shells&amp;quot;, type=float, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;, default=list(io.DEFAULT_SHELLS), help=&amp;quot;shell edges, A (same default in 04-07)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--far&amp;quot;, choices=[&amp;quot;all&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;self&amp;quot;], default=&amp;quot;all&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--lags&amp;quot;, type=float, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;, default=[2.0, 5.0, 10.0], help=&amp;quot;tau values, ps&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--blocks&amp;quot;, type=int, default=io.DEFAULT_BLOCKS)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--own&amp;quot;, choices=[&amp;quot;resident&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;start&amp;quot;], default=&amp;quot;start&amp;quot;,
                    help=&amp;quot;resident: window counts for a shell only if the Li stays in it the whole window; start: shell at t0 (default)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--min-n&amp;quot;, type=float, default=3.0, help=&amp;quot;mean Li in a shell below this: open symbol / flagged in the log&amp;quot;)
    io.add_config_args(ap)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cfg = io.load_config(args.target, args.defaults, args)
    temps = args.T or io.temperatures(cfg)
    outdir = os.path.join(args.outdir, cfg[&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;])
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    edges = np.array(args.shells, float)
    names = io.shell_names(edges)
    K = len(names)
    rows = []
    for T in temps:
        print(f&amp;quot;\n=== {cfg['name']}  T = {T} K ===&amp;quot;)
        li, dops, L, dt = load(cfg, T, args.tmin)
        if not dops:
            raise SystemExit(&amp;quot;no dopant atoms in this system: nothing to compare&amp;quot;)
        F, N = li.shape[:2]
        Lf = np.broadcast_to(L, (F, 3))
        dists = {lab: io.dist_to(li, dop, Lf) for lab, dop in dops}
        dnear = np.min(list(dists.values()), axis=0)
        dists = {&amp;quot;any&amp;quot;: dnear, **dists}
        for tau in args.lags:
            lag = max(1, int(round(tau / dt)))
            if lag &amp;gt;= F - args.blocks * 2:
                print(f&amp;quot;  [skip] tau = {tau} ps is too long for this window&amp;quot;)
                continue
            d = li[lag:] - li[:-lag]
            for lab, d_ in dists.items():
                sh = io.shell_labels(d_, dnear, edges, args.far)
                sh = io.resident_labels(sh, lag) if args.own == &amp;quot;resident&amp;quot; else sh[:-lag]
                NUM, DEN, CNT, ORG = shell_sums(d, sh, K, args.blocks)
                H, Hse = io.jackknife(lambda n_, d_s: n_ / d_s, NUM, DEN)
                nmean = CNT.sum(0) / ORG.sum()
                print(f&amp;quot;  [{lab}] tau = {lag * dt:g} ps    shell      &amp;lt;Li&amp;gt;    H_R +/- jackknife SE&amp;quot;)
                for k in range(K + 1):
                    nm = names[k] if k &amp;lt; K else &amp;quot;all Li&amp;quot;
                    flag = &amp;quot;  &amp;lt;- few Li: H_R -&amp;gt; 1 by construction&amp;quot; if (k &amp;lt; K and nmean[k] &amp;lt; args.min_n) else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;
                    print(f&amp;quot;        {nm:&amp;gt;16} {nmean[k]:8.2f}    {H[k]:6.2f} +/- {Hse[k]:.2f}{flag}&amp;quot;)
                    rows.append(dict(T=T, dopant=lab, tau_ps=lag * dt, shell=nm, n_Li_mean=float(nmean[k]), H_R=float(H[k]),
                                     H_R_se=float(Hse[k]), msd_tr_A2=float(NUM[:, k].sum() / max(CNT[:, k].sum(), 1)),
                                     msd_col_per_Li_A2=float(DEN[:, k].sum() / max(CNT[:, k].sum(), 1)), blocks=args.blocks))
    if not rows:
        raise SystemExit(&amp;quot;nothing computed&amp;quot;)
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, &amp;quot;haven_shell.csv&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;, newline=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;) as fh:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    lags = [t for t in args.lags if any(abs(r[&amp;quot;tau_ps&amp;quot;] - t) &amp;lt; 0.06 for r in rows)]
    plot(rows, lags, names, os.path.join(outdir, &amp;quot;haven_shell.png&amp;quot;), args.min_n)
    print(f&amp;quot;\n[+] {outdir}/haven_shell.csv haven_shell.png haven_shell.svg&amp;quot;)
    print(&amp;quot;Read: H_R(shell) &amp;lt; 1 = the Li that start in this shell move in a correlated way. INTRA-shell only. Open symbols / '&amp;lt;- few Li' = &amp;quot;
          &amp;quot;the number is not usable. Compare with the red 'all Li' line, and use the largest tau; at short tau every H_R -&amp;gt; 1.&amp;quot;)


if __name__ == &amp;quot;__main__&amp;quot;:
    main()
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: llzo_maps.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
llzo_maps.py - shared numerical helpers for 01_density_free_energy.py and 02_compare_temps.py.

  deposit_cic       cloud-in-cell (trilinear) deposition of points on a periodic grid (no nearest-voxel aliasing)
  adaptive_smooth   adaptive-bandwidth Gaussian smoothing (narrow kernels where Li is dense, wide where sparse)
  sphere_values     grid values inside a sphere around a point (periodic)
  apply_shift       periodic roll of a map by a FIXED shift (A), rounded to whole voxels
  project           min projection along an axis (NaN-aware), optionally through a slab around the box centre
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import warnings

import numpy as np

import llzo_svg  # noqa: F401  (every saved .png also gets an editable .svg)
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates

PANELS = [(2, &amp;quot;x&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;y&amp;quot;, 0, 1), (1, &amp;quot;x&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;z&amp;quot;, 0, 2), (0, &amp;quot;y&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;z&amp;quot;, 1, 2)]   # (collapsed axis, xlabel, ylabel, ix, iy)


def deposit_cic(frac, n):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Trilinear (cloud-in-cell) deposit of points frac (M,3) in [0,1) onto a periodic grid n=(nx,ny,nz).
    Voxel i is centred at (i+0.5)/n. Total weight is conserved; result is counts per voxel.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = np.asarray(n, int)
    g = frac * n - 0.5
    i0 = np.floor(g).astype(int)
    d = g - i0
    out = np.zeros(int(np.prod(n)))
    for dx in (0, 1):
        wx = d[:, 0] if dx else 1.0 - d[:, 0]
        ix = (i0[:, 0] + dx) % n[0]
        for dy in (0, 1):
            wy = d[:, 1] if dy else 1.0 - d[:, 1]
            iy = (i0[:, 1] + dy) % n[1]
            for dz in (0, 1):
                wz = d[:, 2] if dz else 1.0 - d[:, 2]
                iz = (i0[:, 2] + dz) % n[2]
                idx = (ix * n[1] + iy) * n[2] + iz
                out += np.bincount(idx, weights=wx * wy * wz, minlength=out.size)
    return out.reshape(tuple(n))


def kernel_voxels(sigma, voxel):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Effective number of voxels under a 3D Gaussian of width sigma (A): (2 sqrt(pi) sigma / voxel)^3.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    return float(np.prod(2.0 * np.sqrt(np.pi) * sigma / np.asarray(voxel)))


def adaptive_smooth(frac, n, voxel, sigma_pilot, s_min, s_max, nclass=6):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Adaptive-bandwidth (Abramson) smoothing. A fixed-sigma pilot density sets, for every sample, a bandwidth
        h_i = sigma_pilot * sqrt( g / rho_pilot(x_i) ),   clipped to [s_min, s_max],   g = geometric mean of rho_pilot
    so samples in dense regions (Li sites) get narrow kernels (peaks are not blurred) and samples in sparse regions
    get wide ones (noise is averaged). Samples are grouped into `nclass` log-spaced bandwidths; each group is
    deposited (CIC) and smoothed once. Returns (smoothed counts/voxel, pilot counts/voxel, cic counts, h_i).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    counts = deposit_cic(frac, n)
    pilot = gaussian_filter(counts, sigma_pilot / voxel, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)
    pv = map_coordinates(pilot, (frac * n - 0.5).T, order=1, mode=&amp;quot;grid-wrap&amp;quot;)
    pv = np.maximum(pv, 1e-12)
    gm = np.exp(np.mean(np.log(pv)))
    h = np.clip(sigma_pilot * np.sqrt(gm / pv), s_min, s_max)
    ks = np.geomspace(s_min, s_max, nclass)
    cls = np.abs(np.log(h)[:, None] - np.log(ks)[None, :]).argmin(axis=1)
    out = np.zeros(tuple(n))
    for k in range(nclass):
        m = cls == k
        if m.any():
            out += gaussian_filter(deposit_cic(frac[m], n), ks[k] / voxel, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)
    return out, pilot, counts, h


def sphere_values(field, L, p, r):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Values of `field` (periodic grid over box L, origin 0) in voxels whose centre is within r (A) of point p.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = np.array(field.shape)
    vox = np.asarray(L) / n
    k = np.ceil(r / vox).astype(int)
    ic = np.floor(np.asarray(p) / vox).astype(int)
    rng = [np.arange(-k[i], k[i] + 1) for i in range(3)]
    sub = field[np.ix_(*[(ic[i] + rng[i]) % n[i] for i in range(3)])]
    off = [(ic[i] + rng[i] + 0.5) * vox[i] - p[i] for i in range(3)]
    d2 = off[0][:, None, None] ** 2 + off[1][None, :, None] ** 2 + off[2][None, None, :] ** 2
    m = d2 &amp;lt; r * r
    return sub[m]


def wrap_track(u, L, nmax=3000):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Unwrapped path u (F,3) -&amp;gt; wrapped path (n,3), every F//nmax-th frame, with a NaN row wherever it crosses a periodic
    boundary (so a plot draws no line across the box). Used to draw how a dopant moves.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    L = np.asarray(L, float)
    u = np.asarray(u, float)
    w = u[::max(1, len(u) // nmax)] % L
    j = np.where(np.any(np.abs(np.diff(w, axis=0)) &amp;gt; L / 2, axis=1))[0]
    return np.insert(w, j + 1, np.nan, axis=0)


def apply_shift(F, L, shift):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Periodic roll of map F by `shift` (A), rounded to whole voxels. Returns (rolled map, shift actually applied, A).
    Use the same `shift` for every temperature of a system so maps stay aligned voxel-for-voxel.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = np.array(F.shape)
    vox = np.asarray(L, float) / n
    sv = np.rint(np.asarray(shift, float) / vox).astype(int)
    return np.roll(F, tuple(sv), axis=(0, 1, 2)), sv * vox


def project(F, axis, slab=0.0, L=None):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;NaN-aware minimum along `axis`. slab &amp;gt; 0: only voxels within +/- slab (A) of the box centre plane
    (meaningful after apply_shift with the dopant at the centre). All-NaN lines give NaN (drawn grey).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    if slab &amp;gt; 0:
        n = F.shape[axis]
        vox = L[axis] / n
        k = int(np.ceil(slab / vox))
        sl = [slice(None)] * 3
        sl[axis] = slice(max(n // 2 - k, 0), n // 2 + k + 1)
        F = F[tuple(sl)]
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter(&amp;quot;ignore&amp;quot;)
        return np.nanmin(F, axis=axis)
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: llzo_sites.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
llzo_sites.py - FIXED Li site list for the garnet analysis, built from the pristine Ia-3d CIF (24d + 96h).

  parse_cif      symmetry operators + atoms of a CIF
  wyckoff_sites  every symmetry-equivalent Li position (24d, 96h, ...) and La/Zr framework positions, tiled to the MD box
  fit_shift      rigid translation that puts the ideal La/Zr framework on the MD time-averaged La/Zr positions
  build          everything 04_sites_hops.py needs, with checks and warnings printed as it goes

Why a fit: the LAMMPS box origin, the framework centre-of-mass drift removal and thermal expansion move the ideal
lattice by a fraction of an Angstrom relative to the MD coordinates. The La/Zr sublattice (which barely moves) is used
as the reference, separately at every temperature.
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import re
import sys
from fractions import Fraction

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.sparse.csgraph import connected_components
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree

KIND = {24: &amp;quot;24d&amp;quot;, 96: &amp;quot;96h&amp;quot;}


def _mi(d):
    return d - np.rint(d)


def _w(x):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;wrap fractional coordinates to [0, 1) (guards the x % 1.0 == 1.0 rounding case).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    x = np.asarray(x, float) % 1.0
    x[x &amp;gt;= 1.0] = 0.0
    return x


def _num(s):
    return float(re.sub(r&amp;quot;\(.*?\)&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;&amp;quot;, s))


def parse_cif(path):
    txt = open(path).read().replace(&amp;quot;\r&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;&amp;quot;)
    ops = []
    for m in re.finditer(r&amp;quot;^\s*\d+\s+'([^']+)'&amp;quot;, txt, re.M):
        R, t = np.zeros((3, 3)), np.zeros(3)
        for i, comp in enumerate(m.group(1).replace(&amp;quot; &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;&amp;quot;).split(&amp;quot;,&amp;quot;)):
            for sgn, tok in re.findall(r&amp;quot;([+-]?)(\d+/\d+|\d+\.?\d*|[xyz])&amp;quot;, comp):
                s = -1.0 if sgn == &amp;quot;-&amp;quot; else 1.0
                if tok in (&amp;quot;x&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;y&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;z&amp;quot;):
                    R[i, &amp;quot;xyz&amp;quot;.index(tok)] += s
                else:
                    t[i] += s * float(Fraction(tok))
        ops.append((R, t))
    if not ops:
        sys.exit(f&amp;quot;{path}: no symmetry operators of the form  N 'x+1/2, y, z'  found&amp;quot;)
    a = _num(re.search(r&amp;quot;_cell_length_a\s+(\S+)&amp;quot;, txt).group(1))
    m = re.search(r&amp;quot;loop_\s*\n((?:\s*_atom_site_\S+[ \t]*\n)+)&amp;quot;, txt)
    if not m:
        sys.exit(f&amp;quot;{path}: no _atom_site loop&amp;quot;)
    cols = re.findall(r&amp;quot;_atom_site_(\S+)&amp;quot;, m.group(1))
    for c in (&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;fract_x&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;fract_y&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;fract_z&amp;quot;):
        if c not in cols:
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;{path}: _atom_site_{c} missing&amp;quot;)
    atoms = []
    for ln in txt[m.end():].splitlines():
        s = ln.split()
        if not s:
            if atoms:
                break
            continue
        if s[0].startswith((&amp;quot;#&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;_&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;loop_&amp;quot;)):
            break
        if len(s) &amp;lt; len(cols):
            continue
        lab = s[cols.index(&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;)]
        sym = s[cols.index(&amp;quot;type_symbol&amp;quot;)] if &amp;quot;type_symbol&amp;quot; in cols else lab
        el = re.match(r&amp;quot;[A-Z][a-z]?&amp;quot;, sym).group(0)
        atoms.append((lab, el, np.array([_num(s[cols.index(k)]) for k in (&amp;quot;fract_x&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;fract_y&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;fract_z&amp;quot;)])))
    return a, ops, atoms


def orbit(ops, p, tol=1e-3):
    out = []
    for R, t in ops:
        q = _w(R @ p + t)
        if not any(np.abs(_mi(q - o)).max() &amp;lt; tol for o in out):
            out.append(q)
    return np.array(out)


def _tile(f, fold):
    fold = np.asarray(fold, int)
    return np.vstack([(f + [i, j, k]) / fold for i in range(fold[0]) for j in range(fold[1]) for k in range(fold[2])])


def wyckoff_sites(cif, fold):
    a, ops, atoms = parse_cif(cif)
    li, fw = [], {}
    for lab, el, p in atoms:
        orb = orbit(ops, p)
        if el == &amp;quot;Li&amp;quot;:
            li.append((lab, orb))
        elif el in (&amp;quot;La&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;Zr&amp;quot;):
            fw[el] = _tile(orb, fold) if el not in fw else np.vstack([fw[el], _tile(orb, fold)])
    # Li orbits that coincide (same Wyckoff position listed twice) are merged
    frac, kind, labs = [], [], []
    for lab, orb in li:
        t = _tile(orb, fold)
        frac.append(t)
        kind += [KIND.get(len(orb), f&amp;quot;{len(orb)}x&amp;quot;)] * len(t)
        labs += [lab] * len(t)
    return dict(a=a, nops=len(ops), frac=np.vstack(frac), kind=np.array(kind), fw=fw,
                orbits=[(lab, len(o)) for lab, o in li])


def fit_shift(md, ideal, L):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;md, ideal: {element: fractional (n,3)}. Returns (t, rms, max, n) with t a fractional translation such that
    ideal + t sits on md. Candidate translations = md atom 0 -&amp;gt; every ideal site of the same element, then refined.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    els = [e for e in ideal if e in md and len(md[e])]
    if not els:
        sys.exit(&amp;quot;no La/Zr found in both the MD and the CIF: cannot align the site list&amp;quot;)

    def match(t):
        d, disp = [], []
        for e in els:
            tree = cKDTree(_w(ideal[e] + t) * L, boxsize=L)
            dd, i = tree.query(_w(md[e]) * L)
            d.append(dd)
            disp.append(_mi(md[e] - (ideal[e][i] + t)))
        return np.concatenate(d), np.vstack(disp)

    cands = md[els[0]][0] - ideal[els[0]]
    t = min(cands, key=lambda c: np.sqrt(np.mean(match(c)[0] ** 2)))
    for _ in range(6):
        t = t + match(t)[1].mean(axis=0)
    d, _ = match(t)
    return _w(t), float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d ** 2))), float(d.max()), len(d)


def _groups(frac, L, R):
    n = len(frac)
    d = np.linalg.norm(_mi(frac[:, None] - frac[None]) * L, axis=-1)
    adj = csr_matrix((d &amp;lt;= R) &amp;amp; (d &amp;gt; 0))
    nc, lab = connected_components(adj, directed=False)
    return [np.where(lab == k)[0] for k in range(nc)]


def _merge(frac, groups):
    out = np.empty((len(groups), 3))
    for k, g in enumerate(groups):
        out[k] = _w(frac[g[0]] + _mi(frac[g] - frac[g[0]]).mean(axis=0))
    return out


def build(cif, data, temps, fold, excl, merge, peaks_frac, warn_rms=0.35, warn_max=1.0):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;data[T] needs: 'L' (3,), 'fw' {El: frac}, 'dops' [{'label','p'(A, relative to box origin)}].
    Returns dict(frac_by_T, frac_ref, kind).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    ws = wyckoff_sites(cif, fold)
    ncell = int(np.prod(fold))
    T0 = temps[0]
    print(f&amp;quot;\n[crystal sites] {cif}&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;  space-group operators: {ws['nops']} (Ia-3d has 96);  Li orbits in the CIF: &amp;quot;
          + &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{lab} -&amp;gt; {n} positions ({KIND.get(n, '?')})&amp;quot; for lab, n in ws[&amp;quot;orbits&amp;quot;]))
    if ws[&amp;quot;nops&amp;quot;] != 96:
        print(&amp;quot;  [WARN] not 96 operators: is this the Ia-3d garnet CIF?&amp;quot;)
    n_cell = sum(n for _, n in ws[&amp;quot;orbits&amp;quot;])
    print(f&amp;quot;  tiled x{tuple(int(x) for x in fold)} = {ncell} conventional cell(s): {len(ws['frac'])} Li sites &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;(expected 120 x {ncell} = {120 * ncell})&amp;quot;)
    if n_cell != 120:
        print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {n_cell} Li positions per cell, expected 24d + 96h = 120&amp;quot;)
    for T in temps:
        rel = data[T][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;] / (ws[&amp;quot;a&amp;quot;] * np.asarray(fold)) - 1.0
        if np.abs(rel).max() &amp;gt; 0.10:
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;  box {data[T]['L']} A at {T} K does not match CIF a = {ws['a']} A x fold {tuple(fold)} &amp;quot;
                     f&amp;quot;(off by {100 * np.abs(rel).max():.0f} %): wrong CIF or wrong fold (set 'fold' in the settings)&amp;quot;)
        if np.abs(rel).max() &amp;gt; 0.04:
            print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] box at {T} K differs {100 * np.abs(rel).max():.1f} % from CIF a x fold (thermal expansion is ~1-2 %)&amp;quot;)

    # ---- per-T alignment on the La/Zr framework
    shift = {}
    for T in temps:
        L = data[T][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;]
        md = data[T][&amp;quot;fw&amp;quot;]
        t, rms, mx, n = fit_shift(md, ws[&amp;quot;fw&amp;quot;], L)
        shift[T] = t
        cnt = &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{e} {len(md.get(e, []))}/{len(ws['fw'][e])}&amp;quot; for e in ws[&amp;quot;fw&amp;quot;])
        print(f&amp;quot;  T = {T} K: framework fit on {n} La/Zr atoms ({cnt} MD/ideal): shift {_mi(t) * L} A, &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;rms residual {rms:.2f} A, max {mx:.2f} A&amp;quot;)
        if len(md.get(&amp;quot;La&amp;quot;, [])) != len(ws[&amp;quot;fw&amp;quot;][&amp;quot;La&amp;quot;]):
            print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {T} K: La count in MD ({len(md.get('La', []))}) != ideal ({len(ws['fw']['La'])}): wrong fold or CIF&amp;quot;)
        if rms &amp;gt; warn_rms or mx &amp;gt; warn_max:
            print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {T} K: framework does not sit on the ideal lattice (rms &amp;gt; {warn_rms} or max &amp;gt; {warn_max} A): &amp;quot;
                  &amp;quot;site assignment unreliable; check the CIF, fold, or whether the structure transformed&amp;quot;)

    frac_T = {T: _w(ws[&amp;quot;frac&amp;quot;] + shift[T]) for T in temps}

    # ---- drop sites occupied by dopants (e.g. Ga on 24d)
    keep = np.ones(len(ws[&amp;quot;frac&amp;quot;]), bool)
    print(f&amp;quot;  sites closer than {excl:g} A to a dopant atom are removed (dopant sits on that Li site):&amp;quot;)
    removed_by_T = {}
    for T in temps:
        L = data[T][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;]
        rem = {}
        for dop in data[T][&amp;quot;dops&amp;quot;]:
            dist = np.linalg.norm(_mi(frac_T[T] - dop[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;] / L) * L, axis=1)
            for i in np.where(dist &amp;lt; excl)[0]:
                rem[i] = (dop[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;], float(dist[i]))
        removed_by_T[T] = rem
    ref = removed_by_T[T0]
    # The framework fit is only defined modulo lattice translations and the I-centring (1/2,1/2,1/2) of Ia-3d: the
    # site SET is unchanged but site INDEX i lands on a different crystal site. Pick, per T, the equivalent shift
    # for which the T0 dopant-occupied index sits on the dopant again, so index i = same crystal site at every T.
    if ref and T0 in data and data[T0][&amp;quot;dops&amp;quot;]:
        fo = np.asarray(fold, int)
        cands = [(np.array([i, j, k]) + 0.5 * h) / fo for i in range(fo[0]) for j in range(fo[1])
                 for k in range(fo[2]) for h in (0, 1)]
        ri = np.array(sorted(ref))
        for T in temps[1:]:
            L = data[T][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;]
            def cost(c):
                f = _w(ws[&amp;quot;frac&amp;quot;][ri] + shift[T] + c)
                return min(np.linalg.norm(_mi(f - dop[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;] / L) * L, axis=1).min() for dop in data[T][&amp;quot;dops&amp;quot;])
            c = min(cands, key=cost)
            if np.abs(c).max() &amp;gt; 0:
                print(f&amp;quot;  T = {T} K: shift moved by {c * L} A (lattice/I-centring equivalent) so site indices match {T0} K&amp;quot;)
            shift[T] = _w(shift[T] + c)
            frac_T[T] = _w(ws[&amp;quot;frac&amp;quot;] + shift[T])
            rem = {}
            for dop in data[T][&amp;quot;dops&amp;quot;]:
                dist = np.linalg.norm(_mi(frac_T[T] - dop[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;] / L) * L, axis=1)
                for i in np.where(dist &amp;lt; excl)[0]:
                    rem[i] = (dop[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;], float(dist[i]))
            removed_by_T[T] = rem
    for i, (lab, dd) in sorted(ref.items()):
        keep[i] = False
        print(f&amp;quot;    site {i} ({ws['kind'][i]}) removed: {dd:.2f} A from {lab}&amp;quot;)
    if not ref:
        print(&amp;quot;    none (dopants are not on Li sites)&amp;quot;)
    for T in temps[1:]:
        if set(removed_by_T[T]) != set(ref):
            print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {T} K: the set of dopant-occupied sites differs from {T0} K (dopant moved?); using the {T0} K set&amp;quot;)
    if len(ref) &amp;gt; 3 * max(1, len(data[T0][&amp;quot;dops&amp;quot;])):
        print(&amp;quot;  [WARN] many sites removed: raise/lower --site-excl?&amp;quot;)

    idx = np.where(keep)[0]
    kind = ws[&amp;quot;kind&amp;quot;][idx]
    frac_T = {T: f[idx] for T, f in frac_T.items()}

    # ---- optional merging of close sites (the 96h pairs are only ~0.8 A apart)
    if merge &amp;gt; 0:
        gr = _groups(frac_T[T0], data[T0][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;], merge)
        sizes = np.bincount([len(g) for g in gr])
        print(f&amp;quot;  --merge-pairs {merge:g} A: {len(idx)} sites -&amp;gt; {len(gr)} sites (group sizes: &amp;quot;
              + &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{n} x {s}&amp;quot; for s, n in enumerate(sizes) if n) + &amp;quot;)&amp;quot;)
        kind = np.array([&amp;quot;/&amp;quot;.join(sorted(set(kind[g]))) + (f&amp;quot; x{len(g)}&amp;quot; if len(g) &amp;gt; 1 else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;) for g in gr])
        frac_T = {T: _merge(f, gr) for T, f in frac_T.items()}

    S = len(kind)
    n_li = None
    print(f&amp;quot;  detected sites: {S}  (expected {120 * ncell} - {int((~keep).sum())} dopant-occupied&amp;quot;
          + (f&amp;quot;, then merged&amp;quot; if merge &amp;gt; 0 else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;) + &amp;quot;)&amp;quot;)
    if merge == 0 and S != 120 * ncell - int((~keep).sum()):
        print(&amp;quot;  [WARN] site count differs from expected&amp;quot;)

    # ---- cross-check against the density peaks (independent of the CIF)
    L = data[T0][&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;]
    if peaks_frac is not None and len(peaks_frac):
        dp = np.linalg.norm(_mi(frac_T[T0][:, None] - peaks_frac[None]) * L, axis=-1)
        a_ = dp.min(axis=1)
        b_ = dp.min(axis=0)
        print(f&amp;quot;  cross-check vs density peaks ({len(peaks_frac)}): {100 * (a_ &amp;lt; 0.5).mean():.0f} % of crystal sites have a peak within 0.5 A &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;(median {np.median(a_):.2f} A); {100 * (b_ &amp;lt; 0.5).mean():.0f} % of peaks have a crystal site within 0.5 A&amp;quot;)
        if (b_ &amp;lt; 0.5).mean() &amp;lt; 0.7:
            print(&amp;quot;  [WARN] many density peaks are far from every crystal site: Li sits at positions the CIF does not have &amp;quot;
                  &amp;quot;(dopant-distorted region? wrong alignment?)&amp;quot;)
    return dict(frac_by_T=frac_T, frac_ref=frac_T[T0], kind=kind)
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: llzo_svg.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
llzo_svg.py - every figure the analysis saves as .png is ALSO saved as an editable .svg next to it.

Open the .svg in Inkscape (File &amp;gt; Open) to move, restyle or delete anything: axes, lines, markers, legends and
all text are separate objects, and the text stays text (svg.fonttype = 'none'; if Inkscape shows another font, the
font named in the file is not installed - pick any font, the layout does not depend on it).
Image-type panels (density / free-energy maps, drawn with imshow / pcolormesh) are embedded as ONE raster picture
inside the .svg; their axes, labels and colourbars are still editable. GIMP opens the .svg too (it rasterises it),
or just open the .png there.

It works by wrapping Figure.savefig once, so no plotting code has to change: importing this module is enough
(llzo_io and llzo_maps import it, so all scripts 01-09 are covered).
Switch off:  LLZO_SVG=0 bash run_pipeline.sh ...      (halves the plotting time of the big sweeps)
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import os

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402,F401
from matplotlib.figure import Figure  # noqa: E402

if os.environ.get(&amp;quot;LLZO_SVG&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;1&amp;quot;) != &amp;quot;0&amp;quot; and not getattr(Figure.savefig, &amp;quot;_llzo_svg&amp;quot;, False):
    matplotlib.rcParams[&amp;quot;svg.fonttype&amp;quot;] = &amp;quot;none&amp;quot;     # keep text as text (editable in Inkscape)
    _orig = Figure.savefig

    def _savefig(self, fname, *args, **kwargs):
        _orig(self, fname, *args, **kwargs)
        if isinstance(fname, (str, os.PathLike)) and str(fname).lower().endswith(&amp;quot;.png&amp;quot;):
            kw = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k not in (&amp;quot;format&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;dpi&amp;quot;)}
            try:
                _orig(self, os.fspath(fname)[:-4] + &amp;quot;.svg&amp;quot;, format=&amp;quot;svg&amp;quot;, **kw)
            except Exception as e:                      # never let the extra copy break an analysis run
                print(f&amp;quot;  [warn] could not write the .svg copy of {fname}: {e}&amp;quot;)

    _savefig._llzo_svg = True
    Figure.savefig = _savefig
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: 01_density_free_energy.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;#!/usr/bin/env python3
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
01_density_free_energy.py - periodic Li density and free-energy map, straight from the dump.

Method
  * Li positions relative to the framework centre of mass (drift removed), wrapped into the box, over the
    analysis window only (t &amp;gt;= tmin).
  * density: cloud-in-cell (trilinear) deposition on a fine grid (default 0.2 A voxels; no nearest-voxel
    aliasing) + ADAPTIVE Gaussian smoothing: narrow kernels (sigma_min) where Li is dense, wide (sigma_max) where
    it is sparse. Compared with one fixed sigma this keeps the site peaks sharp and averages noise only where
    needed, and it reduces the spill-over of site density into empty regions (which scales like kT).
    --no-adaptive gives plain fixed-sigma smoothing (sigma = --sigma).
  * F(r) = -kT ln( rho / rho_uniform ),  rho_uniform = N_Li / V. Zero = uniform Li distribution.
  * sampling mask: a voxel is &amp;quot;sampled&amp;quot; if the fixed-sigma kernel around it holds &amp;gt;= --min-neff raw Li counts.
    Unsampled voxels are NaN (grey in plots), never floored. (The floored F is still written to the .dx/.npz as F.)
  * reference: F_site = median F over the density maxima of the pilot (fixed-sigma) map with rho &amp;gt;= site_ratio *
    rho_uniform. F_rel = F - F_site; F_rel / kT is the temperature-scaled version.
  * uncertainty: the window is split into --nblocks-err blocks; F_err = standard error of ln(rho) over blocks
    times kT. A smoothing-sensitivity number (adaptive vs fixed sigma, median |dF_rel| over the network) is printed.
  * dopants: Li-dopant g(r) (box-average Li density as reference), closest Li approach, and the mean rho/rho_uniform
    in a sphere whose radius is the FIRST MINIMUM of that g(r) (or --rdop), next to a control: the same sphere
    around ordinary Li sites.
  * plots: F_rel (eV) and F_rel/kT, same layout always (full-box min projections along z, y, x). Optional:
      --shift SX SY SZ   fixed periodic shift (A), same for every temperature
      --center Ga | Ga1 | all   one figure per dopant atom, that atom at the box centre (reference position taken from
                          the first temperature processed in this call, so all temperatures share one shift)
      --slab W           with --center: min-project only a +/-W A slab through the centred atom

usage:
  python 01_density_free_energy.py /path/to/folder                     # all temperatures
  python 01_density_free_energy.py /path/to/folder --T 800 1200 --center Ga
  options: --spacing 0.2 --sigma 0.5 --sigma-min 0.25 --sigma-max 0.6 --min-neff 10 --units both|eV|kT
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import argparse
import json
import os
import warnings

import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, maximum_filter

import llzo_io as io
import llzo_maps as mp


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ maps
def _F_from_rho(rho, rho_uni, kT, floor):
    return -kT * np.log(np.maximum(rho, floor * rho_uni) / rho_uni)


def build_maps(li_frac, L, T, args):
    F_, N_ = li_frac.shape[:2]
    n = np.maximum(np.rint(L / args.spacing).astype(int), 4)
    voxel = L / n
    vvol = float(np.prod(voxel))
    pts = li_frac.reshape(-1, 3)
    kT = io.KB_EV * T
    rho_uni = N_ / float(np.prod(L))

    if args.no_adaptive:
        counts = mp.deposit_cic(pts, n)
        pilot = gaussian_filter(counts, args.sigma / voxel, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)
        smoothed = pilot
    else:
        smoothed, pilot, counts, _ = mp.adaptive_smooth(pts, n, voxel, args.sigma, args.sigma_min, args.sigma_max)
    rho = smoothed / (F_ * vvol)
    rho_pilot = pilot / (F_ * vvol)
    F = _F_from_rho(rho, rho_uni, kT, args.floor)

    # sampling mask and resolvable limit (fixed-sigma kernel counts)
    Nk = mp.kernel_voxels(args.sigma, voxel)
    sampled = pilot * Nk &amp;gt;= args.min_neff
    F_thr = -kT * np.log(args.min_neff / (Nk * F_ * vvol) / rho_uni)

    # site reference from the pilot map (smoother =&amp;gt; maxima are not noise-selected)
    size = tuple(int(2 * max(1, round(args.site_radius / v)) + 1) for v in voxel)
    peaks = (rho_pilot == maximum_filter(rho_pilot, size=size, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)) \
        &amp;amp; (rho_pilot &amp;gt;= args.site_ratio * rho_uni) &amp;amp; sampled
    F_site = float(np.median(F[peaks])) if peaks.any() else float(F.min())
    F_rel = np.where(sampled, F - F_site, np.nan)

    # smoothing sensitivity: same pipeline with fixed sigma
    Ff = _F_from_rho(rho_pilot, rho_uni, kT, args.floor)
    Ff_site = float(np.median(Ff[peaks])) if peaks.any() else float(Ff.min())
    Ff_rel = np.where(sampled, Ff - Ff_site, np.nan)
    net = sampled &amp;amp; np.isfinite(F_rel) &amp;amp; (F_rel &amp;lt; 0.3)
    sens = float(np.median(np.abs(F_rel[net] - Ff_rel[net]))) if net.any() and not args.no_adaptive else 0.0

    # block standard error of F
    K = max(2, args.nblocks_err)
    S = np.array([gaussian_filter(mp.deposit_cic(li_frac[idx].reshape(-1, 3), n), args.sigma / voxel, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)
                  for idx in np.array_split(np.arange(F_), K)])
    ok = (S &amp;gt; 0).all(axis=0)
    err = kT * np.log(np.where(S &amp;gt; 0, S, 1.0)).std(axis=0, ddof=1) / np.sqrt(K)
    err = np.where(ok, err, np.nan)
    net2 = net &amp;amp; np.isfinite(err) &amp;amp; (F_rel &amp;lt; 0.2)
    err_med = float(np.median(err[net2])) if net2.any() else float(&amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;)

    site_xyz = (np.argwhere(peaks) + 0.5) * voxel                     # A, relative to box origin
    order = np.argsort(-rho_pilot[peaks])[:500]
    site_xyz = site_xyz[order]
    return dict(counts=counts, rho=rho, rho_pilot=rho_pilot, F=F, F_min0=F - F.min(), F_rel=F_rel, sampled=sampled,
                F_site=F_site, n_sites=int(peaks.sum()), site_xyz=site_xyz, max_resolvable=float(F_thr - F_site),
                n=n, L=L, voxel=voxel, rho_uni=rho_uni, kT=kT, n_frames=F_, n_li=N_, sens=sens, err=err,
                err_med=err_med)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ dopants
def dopant_atoms(cfg, traj, drift):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;One dict per dopant atom: label (element + running index in id order), element, atom id, time-averaged
    position relative to the box origin (A, wrapped), rms displacement about it.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    out, cnt = [], {}
    Lm = traj.L.mean(axis=0)
    for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]:
        idx = np.where(traj.types == t)[0]
        idx = idx[np.argsort(traj.ids[idx])]
        for i in idx:
            el = cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;][t]
            cnt[el] = cnt.get(el, 0) + 1
            series = traj.pos[:, i, :] - drift
            mean = series.mean(axis=0)
            rms = float(np.sqrt(((series - mean) ** 2).sum(-1).mean()))
            out.append(dict(label=f&amp;quot;{el}{cnt[el]}&amp;quot;, element=el, atom_id=int(traj.ids[i]), index=int(i),
                            p=(mean - traj.lo) % Lm, rms=rms, series=series))
    return out


def li_dopant_rdf(li, dser, Lm, dr=0.05):
    rmax = float(Lm.min()) / 2 - 1e-6
    edges = np.arange(0.0, rmax + 0.5 * dr, dr)
    d = li - dser[:, None, :]
    d -= Lm * np.rint(d / Lm)
    r = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1)
    cnt = np.histogram(r.ravel(), edges)[0]
    shell = 4.0 / 3.0 * np.pi * (edges[1:] ** 3 - edges[:-1] ** 3)
    g = cnt / (li.shape[0] * (li.shape[1] / float(np.prod(Lm))) * shell)
    return 0.5 * (edges[1:] + edges[:-1]), g, float(r.min()), float((r &amp;lt; 2.0).sum(axis=1).mean())


def rdf_shells(r, g, r_lo=1.0):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;First peak (g&amp;gt;0.5, r&amp;gt;=r_lo) and the first minimum after it, on a 5-bin smoothed g(r).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    gs = np.convolve(g, np.ones(5) / 5.0, mode=&amp;quot;same&amp;quot;)
    ip = None
    for i in range(1, len(gs) - 1):
        if r[i] &amp;gt;= r_lo and gs[i] &amp;gt; gs[i - 1] and gs[i] &amp;gt;= gs[i + 1] and gs[i] &amp;gt; 0.5:
            ip = i
            break
    if ip is None:
        return None, None
    for j in range(ip + 1, len(gs) - 1):
        if gs[j] &amp;lt; gs[j - 1] and gs[j] &amp;lt;= gs[j + 1]:
            return float(r[ip]), float(r[j])
    return float(r[ip]), None


def dopant_stats(maps, dops, li, Lm, args):
    ratio = maps[&amp;quot;rho&amp;quot;] / maps[&amp;quot;rho_uni&amp;quot;]
    L = maps[&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;]
    res = []
    for d in dops:
        r, g, rmin, n2 = li_dopant_rdf(li, d[&amp;quot;series&amp;quot;], Lm)
        rp, r_first_min = rdf_shells(r, g)
        rs = args.rdop or r_first_min or 3.0
        src = &amp;quot;--rdop&amp;quot; if args.rdop else (&amp;quot;first g(r) minimum&amp;quot; if r_first_min else &amp;quot;fallback 3.0&amp;quot;)
        vals = mp.sphere_values(ratio, L, d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;], rs)
        smp = mp.sphere_values(maps[&amp;quot;sampled&amp;quot;].astype(float), L, d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;], rs)
        # control: same sphere around ordinary Li sites (not within rs of any dopant)
        ctrl = []
        for s in maps[&amp;quot;site_xyz&amp;quot;]:
            far = True
            for d2 in dops:
                dd = s - d2[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;]
                dd -= L * np.rint(dd / L)
                if np.linalg.norm(dd) &amp;lt; rs:
                    far = False
                    break
            if far:
                ctrl.append(float(mp.sphere_values(ratio, L, s, rs).mean()))
        ctrl = np.array(ctrl)
        res.append(dict(label=d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;], element=d[&amp;quot;element&amp;quot;], atom_id=d[&amp;quot;atom_id&amp;quot;], pos_A=[float(x) for x in d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;]],
                        rms_disp_A=d[&amp;quot;rms&amp;quot;], closest_Li_A=rmin, mean_Li_within_2A=n2, r_first_peak=rp,
                        r_first_min=r_first_min, r_sphere=float(rs), r_sphere_source=src,
                        rho_over_uni_sphere=float(vals.mean()), rho_over_uni_min_in_sphere=float(vals.min()),
                        unsampled_frac_in_sphere=float(1.0 - smp.mean()),
                        control_median=float(np.median(ctrl)) if len(ctrl) else float(&amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;),
                        control_p16=float(np.percentile(ctrl, 16)) if len(ctrl) else float(&amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;),
                        control_p84=float(np.percentile(ctrl, 84)) if len(ctrl) else float(&amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;),
                        n_control=int(len(ctrl)), rdf_r=r, rdf_g=g))
    return res


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ plotting
def plot_maps(path, F, L, dops_xyz, title, unit, vmin, vmax, shift=None, slab=0.0, centre=None):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Full-box min-F projections along z, y, x (same layout as always). F is F_rel (eV) or F_rel/kT.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    dops = list(dops_xyz)
    if shift is not None and np.any(np.asarray(shift) != 0):
        F, sh = mp.apply_shift(F, L, shift)
        dops = [(lab, el, (p + sh) % L) for lab, el, p in dops]
    cmap = plt.get_cmap(&amp;quot;viridis_r&amp;quot;).copy()
    cmap.set_bad(&amp;quot;0.82&amp;quot;)
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(15, 4.6), dpi=200)
    colors = {}
    for ax, (axis, xl, yl, ix, iy) in zip(axs, mp.PANELS):
        proj = mp.project(F, axis, slab, L)
        im = ax.imshow(np.ma.masked_invalid(proj).T, origin=&amp;quot;lower&amp;quot;, extent=[0, L[ix], 0, L[iy]], cmap=cmap,
                       vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax, aspect=&amp;quot;equal&amp;quot;)
        for lab, el, p in dops:
            if slab &amp;gt; 0 and abs(p[axis] - L[axis] / 2) &amp;gt; slab + 1.0:
                continue
            c = colors.setdefault(el, [&amp;quot;red&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;orange&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;cyan&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;magenta&amp;quot;][len(colors) % 5])
            big = lab == centre
            ax.scatter(p[ix], p[iy], s=130 if big else 70, marker=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;, color=c, edgecolor=&amp;quot;k&amp;quot;,
                       linewidth=1.0 if big else 0.6, label=el, zorder=5)
        ax.set_xlabel(f&amp;quot;{xl} (A)&amp;quot;)
        ax.set_ylabel(f&amp;quot;{yl} (A)&amp;quot;)
        ax.set_title(f&amp;quot;min-F in +/-{slab:g} A slab normal to {'xyz'[axis]}&amp;quot; if slab &amp;gt; 0
                     else f&amp;quot;min-F projection along {'xyz'[axis]}&amp;quot;)
    h, l = axs[0].get_legend_handles_labels()
    if h:
        u = dict(zip(l, h))
        axs[0].legend(u.values(), u.keys(), loc=&amp;quot;upper right&amp;quot;, fontsize=8, title=&amp;quot;dopants&amp;quot;, framealpha=0.85)
    fig.colorbar(im, ax=axs, shrink=0.85,
                 label=(&amp;quot;F - F_site (eV)&amp;quot; if unit == &amp;quot;eV&amp;quot; else &amp;quot;(F - F_site) / kT&amp;quot;) + &amp;quot;   [grey = not sampled]&amp;quot;)
    sub = []
    if centre:
        sub.append(f&amp;quot;centred on {centre}&amp;quot;)
    elif shift is not None and np.any(np.asarray(shift) != 0):
        sub.append(f&amp;quot;periodic shift ({shift[0]:g}, {shift[1]:g}, {shift[2]:g}) A&amp;quot;)
    fig.suptitle(title + (&amp;quot;\n&amp;quot; + &amp;quot;; &amp;quot;.join(sub) if sub else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;), y=1.06 if sub else 1.0)
    fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches=&amp;quot;tight&amp;quot;)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_rdf(path, stats, title):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.5, 4.2), dpi=150)
    for s in stats:
        ln, = ax.plot(s[&amp;quot;rdf_r&amp;quot;], s[&amp;quot;rdf_g&amp;quot;], label=f&amp;quot;{s['label']}  (closest Li {s['closest_Li_A']:.2f} A)&amp;quot;)
        ax.axvline(s[&amp;quot;r_sphere&amp;quot;], color=ln.get_color(), ls=&amp;quot;:&amp;quot;, lw=1)
    ax.axhline(1, color=&amp;quot;0.6&amp;quot;, lw=0.6)
    ax.set_xlabel(&amp;quot;r (A)&amp;quot;)
    ax.set_ylabel(&amp;quot;g_Li-dopant(r)  (vs box-average Li density)&amp;quot;)
    ax.set_title(title + &amp;quot;  (dotted: sphere radius used)&amp;quot;)
    ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path)
    plt.close(fig)


def write_vmd_script(path, T):
    with open(path, &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;) as f:
        f.write(f&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;# VMD: load Li density / free energy from 01_density_free_energy.py (T = {T} K)
# usage:  vmd -e load_T{T}.tcl        (isovalues are starting points - adjust in Graphics &amp;gt; Representations)
mol new rho_T{T}K.dx type dx waitfor all
mol delrep 0 top
mol representation Isosurface 0.002 0 0 0 1 1
mol color ColorID 1
mol addrep top
mol new Fmin0_T{T}K.dx type dx waitfor all
mol delrep 0 top
mol representation Isosurface 0.15 0 0 0 1 1
mol color ColorID 4
mol addrep top
pbc box -center origin
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;)


def edge_note(dops, L, margin=1.5):
    bad, sug = [], np.zeros(3)
    for d in dops:
        dm = np.minimum(d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;], L - d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;])
        for k in range(3):
            if dm[k] &amp;lt; margin:
                bad.append(f&amp;quot;{d['label']} ({'xyz'[k]} = {d['p'][k]:.2f})&amp;quot;)
                sug[k] = round(L[k] / 2, 1)
    if bad:
        print(f&amp;quot;  [note] {', '.join(sorted(set(bad)))} within {margin} A of a cell edge: cut in the plots. &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;Use the same --shift {sug[0]:g} {sug[1]:g} {sug[2]:g} for every T, or --center &amp;lt;element|label&amp;gt;.&amp;quot;)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ driver
DEFAULT_RANGES = {&amp;quot;site&amp;quot;: dict(eV=(-0.1, 0.6), kT=(-1.0, 6.0)),
                  &amp;quot;min&amp;quot;: dict(eV=(0.0, 0.6), kT=(0.0, 8.0)),
                  &amp;quot;uniform&amp;quot;: dict(eV=(-0.3, 0.6), kT=(-3.0, 6.0))}


def run_one(cfg, T, args, REF):
    path = args.dump or io.dump_path(cfg, T)
    print(f&amp;quot;\n=== {cfg['name']}  T = {T} K ===&amp;quot;)
    traj = io.prepare(cfg, path, args.tmin)
    lm = io.li_mask(cfg, traj)
    drift = io.framework_drift(cfg, traj)
    li = traj.pos[:, lm, :] - drift[:, None, :]
    frac = io.frac_wrapped(li, traj.lo, traj.L)
    L = traj.L.mean(axis=0)
    maps = build_maps(frac, L, T, args)
    dops = dopant_atoms(cfg, traj, drift)
    stats = dopant_stats(maps, dops, li, L, args) if dops else []
    for d in dops:
        if d[&amp;quot;rms&amp;quot;] &amp;gt; 1.0:
            print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {d['label']} moves {d['rms']:.2f} A rms about its mean position: centring on it / its &amp;quot;
                  f&amp;quot;time-averaged position is not meaningful&amp;quot;)
    outdir = os.path.join(args.outdir, cfg[&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;])
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    tag = f&amp;quot;T{T}K&amp;quot;
    kT = maps[&amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;]

    rdf_kw = {&amp;quot;rdf_r&amp;quot;: stats[0][&amp;quot;rdf_r&amp;quot;]} if stats else {}
    rdf_kw.update({f&amp;quot;rdf_g_{s['label']}&amp;quot;: s[&amp;quot;rdf_g&amp;quot;] for s in stats})
    np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;density_F_{tag}.npz&amp;quot;), rho=maps[&amp;quot;rho&amp;quot;], F=maps[&amp;quot;F&amp;quot;],
                        F_min0=maps[&amp;quot;F_min0&amp;quot;], F_rel=maps[&amp;quot;F_rel&amp;quot;], F_err=maps[&amp;quot;err&amp;quot;], sampled=maps[&amp;quot;sampled&amp;quot;],
                        F_site=maps[&amp;quot;F_site&amp;quot;], max_resolvable=maps[&amp;quot;max_resolvable&amp;quot;], counts=maps[&amp;quot;counts&amp;quot;], L=maps[&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;],
                        lo=traj.lo, T=T, rho_uniform=maps[&amp;quot;rho_uni&amp;quot;], sigma=args.sigma, spacing=args.spacing, **rdf_kw)
    io.write_dx(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;rho_{tag}.dx&amp;quot;), maps[&amp;quot;rho&amp;quot;], traj.lo, maps[&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;], f&amp;quot;Li number density (A^-3), T={T} K&amp;quot;)
    io.write_dx(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;Fmin0_{tag}.dx&amp;quot;), maps[&amp;quot;F_min0&amp;quot;], traj.lo, maps[&amp;quot;L&amp;quot;], f&amp;quot;F - Fmin (eV), T={T} K&amp;quot;)
    write_vmd_script(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;load_{tag}.tcl&amp;quot;), T)

    # ---- plots
    ref = args.plot_ref
    F_eV = {&amp;quot;uniform&amp;quot;: maps[&amp;quot;F&amp;quot;], &amp;quot;min&amp;quot;: maps[&amp;quot;F_min0&amp;quot;], &amp;quot;site&amp;quot;: maps[&amp;quot;F_rel&amp;quot;]}[ref]
    rg = DEFAULT_RANGES[ref]
    vm = {&amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;: (args.vmin if args.vmin is not None else rg[&amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;][0], args.vmax if args.vmax is not None else rg[&amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;][1]),
          &amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;: (args.vmin_kT if args.vmin_kT is not None else rg[&amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;][0],
                 args.vmax_kT if args.vmax_kT is not None else rg[&amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;][1])}
    units = [&amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;] if args.units == &amp;quot;both&amp;quot; else [args.units]
    smooth = f&amp;quot;sigma {args.sigma} A&amp;quot; if args.no_adaptive else f&amp;quot;adaptive sigma {args.sigma_min}-{args.sigma_max} A&amp;quot;
    title = (f&amp;quot;{cfg['name']}  T = {T} K   (window {traj.time_ps[0]:.0f}-{traj.time_ps[-1]:.0f} ps, &amp;quot;
             f&amp;quot;{maps['n_frames']} frames, {maps['n_li']} Li, {args.spacing} A voxels, {smooth})&amp;quot;)

    views = [(None, args.shift)]
    centred = False
    if args.center:
        if not dops:
            print(&amp;quot;  [center] --center ignored: no dopant atoms in this system; plotting the unshifted maps&amp;quot;)
        else:
            pick = [d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;] for d in dops if args.center in (d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;], d[&amp;quot;element&amp;quot;]) or args.center == &amp;quot;all&amp;quot;]
            if not pick:
                print(f&amp;quot;  [center] --center {args.center} ignored: no such dopant. Available: {[d['label'] for d in dops]}; &amp;quot;
                      &amp;quot;plotting the unshifted maps&amp;quot;)
            else:
                centred = True
                if not REF:
                    REF.update({d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;]: d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;].copy() for d in dops})
                    print(f&amp;quot;  [center] reference positions taken from T = {T} K; the same shift is used for every T in this call&amp;quot;)
                own = {d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;]: d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;] for d in dops}
                views = [(lab, L / 2 - REF.get(lab, own[lab])) for lab in pick]
    if not centred and not args.shift and dops:
        edge_note(dops, L)
    xyz = [(d[&amp;quot;label&amp;quot;], d[&amp;quot;element&amp;quot;], d[&amp;quot;p&amp;quot;]) for d in dops]
    for lab, shift in views:
        sfx = f&amp;quot;_{lab}&amp;quot; if lab else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;
        if &amp;quot;eV&amp;quot; in units:
            plot_maps(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;F_projections_{tag}{sfx}.png&amp;quot;), F_eV, L, xyz, title, &amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;, *vm[&amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;],
                      shift=shift, slab=args.slab if lab else 0.0, centre=lab)
        if &amp;quot;kT&amp;quot; in units:
            plot_maps(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;F_projections_kT_{tag}{sfx}.png&amp;quot;), F_eV / kT, L, xyz, title, &amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;,
                      *vm[&amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;], shift=shift, slab=args.slab if lab else 0.0, centre=lab)
    if stats:
        plot_rdf(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;dopant_rdf_{tag}.png&amp;quot;), stats, f&amp;quot;{cfg['name']} T = {T} K&amp;quot;)

    per_voxel = maps[&amp;quot;n_frames&amp;quot;] * maps[&amp;quot;n_li&amp;quot;] / maps[&amp;quot;counts&amp;quot;].size
    summary = dict(system=cfg[&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;], T=T, frames=maps[&amp;quot;n_frames&amp;quot;], n_li=maps[&amp;quot;n_li&amp;quot;],
                   window_ps=[float(traj.time_ps[0]), float(traj.time_ps[-1])], grid=[int(x) for x in maps[&amp;quot;n&amp;quot;]],
                   voxel_A=[float(x) for x in maps[&amp;quot;voxel&amp;quot;]], sigma_A=args.sigma, adaptive=not args.no_adaptive,
                   sigma_min_A=args.sigma_min, sigma_max_A=args.sigma_max, kT_eV=kT,
                   rho_uniform_per_A3=maps[&amp;quot;rho_uni&amp;quot;], mean_raw_counts_per_voxel=per_voxel,
                   F_range_eV=[float(maps[&amp;quot;F&amp;quot;].min()), float(maps[&amp;quot;F&amp;quot;].max())], F_site_ref_eV=maps[&amp;quot;F_site&amp;quot;],
                   n_site_maxima=maps[&amp;quot;n_sites&amp;quot;], sampled_fraction=float(maps[&amp;quot;sampled&amp;quot;].mean()),
                   max_resolvable_dF_eV=maps[&amp;quot;max_resolvable&amp;quot;], min_neff=args.min_neff,
                   smoothing_sensitivity_eV=maps[&amp;quot;sens&amp;quot;], F_err_median_network_eV=maps[&amp;quot;err_med&amp;quot;],
                   dopants=[{k: v for k, v in s.items() if k not in (&amp;quot;rdf_r&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;rdf_g&amp;quot;)} for s in stats])
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;density_summary_{tag}.json&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;) as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)
    print(f&amp;quot;  grid {tuple(int(x) for x in maps['n'])}, mean raw counts/voxel = {per_voxel:.2f}&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;  F_site = {maps['F_site']:.3f} eV = {maps['F_site'] / kT:.2f} kT (median over {maps['n_sites']} density maxima);&amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot; rho_site/rho_uniform = {np.exp(-maps['F_site'] / kT):.1f}&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;  sampled volume {100 * summary['sampled_fraction']:.0f}%; barriers resolvable up to ~{maps['max_resolvable']:.2f} eV &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;({maps['max_resolvable'] / kT:.1f} kT) above a site (grey = beyond that)&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;  sampling limit: voxel is sampled if the sigma = {args.sigma} A kernel holds &amp;gt;= {args.min_neff} raw counts -&amp;gt; &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;voxels with Li density below {100 * np.exp(-maps['max_resolvable'] / kT):.2f} % of a site are grey &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;(ln(rho_site/rho_thr) = {maps['max_resolvable'] / kT:.2f} kT)&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;  F uncertainty (block SE, network voxels) ~ {maps['err_med']:.3f} eV; smoothing sensitivity &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;(adaptive vs fixed sigma) ~ {maps['sens']:.3f} eV&amp;quot;)
    fmt = lambda x: &amp;quot;-&amp;quot; if x is None else f&amp;quot;{x:.2f}&amp;quot;
    for s in stats:
        print(f&amp;quot;  {s['label']}: closest Li {s['closest_Li_A']:.2f} A, &amp;lt;Li within 2 A&amp;gt; = {s['mean_Li_within_2A']:.2f}; &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;g(r) first peak {fmt(s['r_first_peak'])} A, first min {fmt(s['r_first_min'])} A -&amp;gt; sphere r = {s['r_sphere']:.2f} A &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;({s['r_sphere_source']}): rho/rho_uni = {s['rho_over_uni_sphere']:.2f} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;(control Li-site spheres {s['control_median']:.2f}, {s['control_p16']:.2f}-{s['control_p84']:.2f}, &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;n={s['n_control']}); unsampled in sphere {100 * s['unsampled_frac_in_sphere']:.0f}%&amp;quot;)
    if per_voxel &amp;lt; 0.2:
        print(&amp;quot;  [note] sparse raw sampling per voxel: rely on the smoothed map&amp;quot;)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;target&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;folder with the dumps + element_list (or a settings .yaml whose dump_dir points there)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--defaults&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;settings file with run_steps, tmin_ps, ... (default: analysis_defaults.yaml)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--T&amp;quot;, type=int, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dump&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;explicit dump file (only with a single --T)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--spacing&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.2, help=&amp;quot;voxel size, A (default 0.2)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--sigma&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.5, help=&amp;quot;pilot / fixed Gaussian sigma, A (default 0.5)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--no-adaptive&amp;quot;, action=&amp;quot;store_true&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;plain fixed-sigma smoothing instead of adaptive&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--sigma-min&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.25, help=&amp;quot;adaptive: narrowest kernel, A (default 0.25)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--sigma-max&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.6, help=&amp;quot;adaptive: widest kernel, A (default 0.6)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--floor&amp;quot;, type=float, default=1e-4, help=&amp;quot;rho floor as fraction of uniform (only for the floored F)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--min-neff&amp;quot;, type=float, default=10.0,
                    help=&amp;quot;min raw counts inside the fixed-sigma kernel for a voxel to count as sampled (default 10)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--site-ratio&amp;quot;, type=float, default=3.0, help=&amp;quot;site maxima need rho &amp;gt;= this x uniform (default 3)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--site-radius&amp;quot;, type=float, default=1.0, help=&amp;quot;A; maxima are local within this radius (default 1.0)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--nblocks-err&amp;quot;, type=int, default=4, help=&amp;quot;time blocks for the F standard error (default 4)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--rdop&amp;quot;, type=float, help=&amp;quot;sphere radius (A) around dopants; default: first g(r) minimum&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--plot-ref&amp;quot;, choices=[&amp;quot;site&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;min&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;uniform&amp;quot;], default=&amp;quot;site&amp;quot;,
                    help=&amp;quot;zero of the plotted F: 'site' (default, typical Li site), 'min' (deepest voxel), 'uniform'&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--units&amp;quot;, choices=[&amp;quot;both&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;eV&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;kT&amp;quot;], default=&amp;quot;both&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;plot F in eV, in kT, or both (default)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--vmin&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--vmax&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--vmin-kT&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--vmax-kT&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--shift&amp;quot;, type=float, nargs=3, metavar=(&amp;quot;SX&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;SY&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;SZ&amp;quot;),
                    help=&amp;quot;fixed periodic shift (A) for the plotted map and dopants; same values for every T&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--center&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;dopant element (Ga), atom label (Ga1) or 'all': one figure per atom, centred&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--slab&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.0, help=&amp;quot;with --center: half-thickness (A) of a slab projection&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--tmin&amp;quot;, type=float, help=&amp;quot;override analysis-window start (ps)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--outdir&amp;quot;, default=&amp;quot;results&amp;quot;)
    io.add_config_args(ap)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.slab &amp;gt; 0 and not args.center:
        ap.error(&amp;quot;--slab needs --center&amp;quot;)
    cfg = io.load_config(args.target, args.defaults, args)
    temps = args.T or io.temperatures(cfg)
    if args.dump and len(temps) != 1:
        ap.error(&amp;quot;--dump needs exactly one --T&amp;quot;)
    REF = {}
    for T in temps:
        run_one(cfg, T, args, REF)


if __name__ == &amp;quot;__main__&amp;quot;:
    warnings.filterwarnings(&amp;quot;ignore&amp;quot;, category=RuntimeWarning)
    main()
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: 04_sites_hops.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;#!/usr/bin/env python3
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
04_sites_hops.py - Li sites, hops, concerted hops and site-to-site barriers, straight from the dumps.

Pipeline (framework-COM drift removed first, same as 00-03)
  1 sites     Peaks of the Li density pooled over ALL temperatures (one site list per composition, so every T and every
              dopant concentration can be compared site by site). Peaks closer than --merge A are merged.
              --sites-cif CIF: use FIXED crystal sites instead (24d + 96h from the Ia-3d CIF, tiled to the box, aligned
              per T on the La/Zr framework, dopant-occupied sites removed, --merge-pairs optional). Peaks are then only
              a cross-check.
  2 assign    Every Li in every frame -&amp;gt; nearest site (periodic k-d tree). A hop is committed only when the Li has
              stayed at the new site for --min-res ps (default 0.3 ps); shorter visits are vibration/transit, not hops.
  3 hops      Event = (Li, time, site_from, site_to, jump length, dwell time at site_from).
              Rates per site pair: Gamma_ab = n_ab / (Li-time spent on a).
  4 shells    Hop rate, back-hop % and concerted fraction per distance shell (--shells, default 3 5 7 A -&amp;gt; 0-3, 3-5, 5-7, &amp;gt;7;
              far = &amp;gt;7 A from EVERY dopant, --far self = from that dopant only). TWO methods, both reported for 'any'
              (nearest dopant) and for each dopant alone (same code and same definitions as 06, see llzo_io.py):
                site_left : shell = distance of the SITE the Li leaves (fixed site list) to the dopant AT THE HOP FRAME (dopant moves)
                li_at_hop : shell = distance of the Li itself to the dopant in the hop frame (instantaneous)
              Error bars everywhere = delete-one-block jackknife over --blocks time blocks. (No dopant -&amp;gt; one shell.)
  5 concerted Two hops of DIFFERENT Li are coupled if they start within --tc ps and their sites are within --rc A
              (any of the 4 site pairs). Coupled hops are chained into clusters; size &amp;gt;= 2 = concerted.
              Also: 'refill' = fraction of hops whose vacated site is entered by another Li within --tc ps.
              CONTROL: every Li's hop times are shifted by an independent random circular offset (--nshuf times). This keeps
              each Li's own rate and the spatial distribution of hops but removes cross-Li timing, so the shuffled
              fractions are the chance level. Excess = observed - chance, z = excess / sd(chance).
  6 barriers  (needs results/&amp;lt;system&amp;gt;/density_F_T*K.npz from 01) For every site pair with &amp;gt;= --nmin observed hops:
              bottleneck (minimax) path of F_rel inside a tube of radius --tube A around the straight line a-b;
              barrier_ab = max_path F - F_a. Also nu_eff = Gamma_ab exp(barrier/kT): should be ~ phonon frequency
              (1-10 THz) if the F-barrier and the observed rate are consistent.

Limits you should know:
  * Time resolution = dump spacing (0.1 ps): hops faster than that are seen as one frame, --tc below ~0.3 ps means nothing.
  * F is a smoothed, Li-Li-correlated free energy, not a single-particle NEB barrier; it is a lower bound where smoothing
    raises the density at a saddle. Pairs not connected inside sampled voxels are reported as blocked (barrier &amp;gt;= limit).
  * Concerted fraction has binomial error ~ sqrt(f(1-f)/n_hops) (hops are not fully independent -&amp;gt; optimistic).

usage:
  python 04_sites_hops.py /path/to/folder                       # all temperatures found in the folder
  python 04_sites_hops.py /path/to/folder --T 800 1200 --tc 0.5 --rc 3.5 --min-res 0.3
  python 04_sites_hops.py /path/to/folder --sites-cif cubic_LLZO_wyckoff.cif [--merge-pairs 1.0] [--site-excl 1.0]
  python 04_sites_hops.py /path/to/folder --no-barriers         # skip step 6 (no 01 results needed)
  python 04_sites_hops.py /path/to/folder --sites-cif X.cif --net-center all --net-slab 2 --net-window 9 \\
         [--net-label occ|hops|index|both|none] [--net-bg hopped|geom|none] [--net-wref N] [--bond-cut 2.6]
  Network plots: line width saturates at --net-wref hops (default: 95th percentile of the hopped pairs) so a few flickering pairs
  next to a dopant do not shrink every other bond to a hair; the legend says when it is clipped.  Faint dashed lines = bonds that
  hopped at another T (or, with --net-bg geom, every pair closer than --bond-cut) but not at this T.  Numbers on the sites = mean
  Li on the site (--net-label).  The log prints hop coverage and the busiest bonds with their back-hop share.
outputs (results/&amp;lt;system&amp;gt;/): sites.csv, hops_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv, hop_summary.csv, barriers_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv,
                             hop_shell_stats.csv, back_hop_shell.png, hop_rate_ratio_shell.png, nonback_ratio_shell.png
                             (rows: 'any' + each dopant, columns: the two methods, ONE shared y axis per figure),
                             hops_summary.png, hop_network_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.png,
                             site_activity_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv (per-site Li, hops, rate, distance to each dopant),
                             state_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.npz (committed site of every Li in every frame; read by 06)
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import argparse
import csv
import os
from collections import Counter

import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import generate_binary_structure, gaussian_filter, label, maximum_filter
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree
from scipy.stats import linregress, spearmanr

import llzo_io as io
import llzo_maps as mp
import llzo_sites as ls


GLOSSARY = &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
HOW TO READ THIS LOG (also saved as hop_glossary.txt)
 site        density peak; a Li 'sits' on its nearest site. hop = Li stays &amp;gt;= --min-res ps on a new site.
 dwell       time between two hops of the same Li.  High back-hop % = Li rattles between two neighbouring sites;
             the hop rate then overstates net transport.
 shell rate  hops of a shell / (Li-time in that shell) = 'per Li per ps'. Two methods (see step 4): site_left / li_at_hop.
             1/rate = mean time a Li stays before leaving. Rate near dopant &amp;lt; rate far = dopant slows hopping.
 back-hop %  hops that undo the same Li's previous hop / hops that HAVE a previous hop (a Li's first hop cannot be one).
 non-back    hops that do not undo the previous hop; its rate is closer to net transport than the raw rate.
 error bar   delete-one-block jackknife over --blocks contiguous time blocks; &amp;lt; 2 SE = noise; hops of one Li are correlated.
 CHANCE      what you would see if every Li hopped independently. Made by giving each Li's hop times a random
             circular time shift (keeps each Li's own rate and where it hops, destroys who-hops-with-whom).
             Repeated --nshuf times: mean = chance level, sd = its noise.
 EXCESS      observed - chance. Normalised: (obs-chance)/(1-chance) = of the hops that would be lonely by chance,
             the share that instead have a partner = genuine coupling.  z = excess/sd(chance); z &amp;lt; ~3 = noise.
 concerted   a hop of ANOTHER Li starts within --tc ps and its sites are within --rc A of ours.
 cluster     hops chained by that rule (A~B, B~C -&amp;gt; one cluster). Chains grow with hop density, so the LARGEST
             cluster is NOT 'N Li moving together'; compare it with its shuffled value.
 refill      a vacated site is entered by another Li within +/- tc. Chance ~ 1-exp(-arrivals per site per ps x window).
 barrier     F_saddle - F_start on the F map (F = -kT ln rho/rho_site). Directional (a-&amp;gt;b differs from b-&amp;gt;a).
 nu_eff      rate / exp(-barrier/kT). Transition-state theory: rate = nu exp(-barrier/kT), nu ~ 1-10 THz (rule of
             thumb, order of magnitude). nu_eff &amp;lt;&amp;lt; 1 THz: F-barrier too low (smoothing fills saddles) -&amp;gt; rank pairs,
             do not quote as absolute Ea.
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
def wrap_pos(pos, lo, L):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Cartesian coordinates in [0, L) relative to the box origin. pos (F,N,3).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    w = (pos - lo) % L
    return np.where(w &amp;gt;= L, w - L, w)


def mi(d, L):
    return d - L * np.rint(d / L)


def load_T(cfg, T, tmin):
    traj = io.prepare(cfg, io.dump_path(cfg, T), tmin)
    lm = io.li_mask(cfg, traj)
    drift = io.framework_drift(cfg, traj)
    li = traj.pos[:, lm, :] - drift[:, None, :]
    L = traj.L.mean(axis=0)
    dops = []
    cnt = {}
    for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]:
        for i in np.where(traj.types == t)[0]:
            el = cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;][t]
            cnt[el] = cnt.get(el, 0) + 1
            p = ((traj.pos[:, i, :] - drift).mean(axis=0) - traj.lo) % L
            dops.append(dict(label=f&amp;quot;{el}{cnt[el]}&amp;quot;, p=p, x=io.dopant_xyz(cfg, traj, drift, i),    # x: per frame, smoothed over --dop-smooth-ps (shells)
                             u=traj.pos[:, i, :] - drift - traj.lo))                       # u: RAW path relative to the box origin (plots, rms)
    fw = {}                                           # time-mean La/Zr positions (fractional): reference for crystal sites
    for el in (&amp;quot;La&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;Zr&amp;quot;):
        m_ = np.isin(traj.types, [t_ for t_, e_ in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items() if e_ == el])
        if m_.any():
            fw[el] = (((traj.pos[:, m_, :] - drift[:, None, :]).mean(axis=0) - traj.lo) / L) % 1.0
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(traj.time_ps)))
    return dict(T=T, fw=fw, li=li, lo=traj.lo, L=L, Lf=np.asarray(traj.L, float), dt=dt, window=float(traj.time_ps[-1] - traj.time_ps[0]),
                dops=dops, fold=io.get_fold(cfg, L), time=traj.time_ps)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 1 sites
def find_sites(frac_all, Lref, spacing, sigma, radius, ratio, merge):
    n = np.maximum(np.rint(Lref / spacing).astype(int), 4)
    vox = Lref / n
    counts = mp.deposit_cic(frac_all, n)
    rho = gaussian_filter(counts, sigma / vox, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)
    mean = counts.sum() / counts.size
    size = tuple(int(2 * max(1, round(radius / v)) + 1) for v in vox)
    pk = (rho == maximum_filter(rho, size=size, mode=&amp;quot;wrap&amp;quot;)) &amp;amp; (rho &amp;gt;= ratio * mean)
    idx = np.argwhere(pk)
    dens = rho[pk]
    order = np.argsort(-dens)
    xyz = (idx[order] + 0.5) * vox
    keep = []
    tree_pts = []
    for p in xyz:                                     # greedy merge of near-duplicate peaks (plateaus)
        if tree_pts and np.min(np.linalg.norm(mi(np.array(tree_pts) - p, Lref), axis=1)) &amp;lt; merge:
            continue
        keep.append(p)
        tree_pts.append(p)
    return np.array(keep) / Lref                      # fractional coordinates


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 2/3 assignment and hops
def assign(li, lo, L, sites):
    F, N, _ = li.shape
    w = wrap_pos(li, lo, L).reshape(-1, 3)
    tree = cKDTree(sites % L, boxsize=L)
    d, s = tree.query(w, k=2)
    return s[:, 0].reshape(F, N), d[:, 0].reshape(F, N), (d[:, 1] - d[:, 0]).reshape(F, N)


def commit_hops(s, m):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Per Li: hop committed when the nearest site differs from the current one for m consecutive frames.
    Returns list of (frame_of_first_frame_at_new_site, site_from, site_to) and the committed state per frame.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    F, N = s.shape
    events, state = [], np.empty_like(s)
    for i in range(N):
        col = s[:, i]
        cur, cand, cnt, cand_start = col[0], -1, 0, 0
        last = 0
        seg = []                                      # (start_frame, site)
        seg.append((0, cur))
        for f in range(1, F):
            x = col[f]
            if x == cur:
                cand, cnt = -1, 0
                continue
            if x == cand:
                cnt += 1
            else:
                cand, cnt, cand_start = x, 1, f
            if cnt &amp;gt;= m:
                events.append((i, cand_start, cur, cand))
                seg.append((cand_start, cand))
                cur, cand, cnt = cand, -1, 0
        for k, (f0, site) in enumerate(seg):
            f1 = seg[k + 1][0] if k + 1 &amp;lt; len(seg) else F
            state[f0:f1, i] = site
    events.sort(key=lambda e: (e[1], e[0]))
    return events, state


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 5 concerted hops
def cluster_sizes(f, li, a, b, win, near):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Chain hops of different Li that start within `win` frames and whose sites are neighbours. Returns cluster size
    of every hop.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = len(f)
    order = np.argsort(f, kind=&amp;quot;stable&amp;quot;)
    fo, lo_, ao, bo = f[order].tolist(), li[order].tolist(), a[order].tolist(), b[order].tolist()
    parent = list(range(n))

    def find(x):
        while parent[x] != x:
            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]
            x = parent[x]
        return x

    for i in range(n):
        j = i + 1
        while j &amp;lt; n and fo[j] - fo[i] &amp;lt;= win:
            if lo_[j] != lo_[i] and (near[ao[i], ao[j]] or near[ao[i], bo[j]] or near[bo[i], ao[j]] or near[bo[i], bo[j]]):
                ri, rj = find(i), find(j)
                if ri != rj:
                    parent[ri] = rj
            j += 1
    roots = [find(i) for i in range(n)]
    c = Counter(roots)
    size = np.empty(n, int)
    size[order] = [c[r] for r in roots]
    return size


def refill_flags(f, li, a, b, win):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;True for hops i (a-&amp;gt;b) such that another Li enters site a within +-win frames.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    arr = {}
    for k in range(len(f)):
        arr.setdefault(b[k], []).append((f[k], li[k]))
    arr = {s: sorted(v) for s, v in arr.items()}
    out = np.zeros(len(f), bool)
    for k in range(len(f)):
        for fj, lj in arr.get(a[k], []):
            if lj != li[k] and abs(fj - f[k]) &amp;lt;= win:
                out[k] = True
                break
    return out


def concerted_stats(ev, F, win, near, shell_of_event, nshuf, seed, nshell=None):
    f, li, a, b = ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;li&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;a&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;b&amp;quot;]
    n = len(f)
    res = dict(n=n)
    if n &amp;lt; 5:
        return None
    size = cluster_sizes(f, li, a, b, win, near)
    ref = refill_flags(f, li, a, b, win)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    N = int(li.max()) + 1
    fc, fr, sh, mx, shr = [], [], [], [], []
    nsh = max(int(shell_of_event.max()) + 1, nshell or 0)
    for _ in range(nshuf):
        off = rng.integers(0, F, N)
        fs = (f + off[li]) % F
        sz = cluster_sizes(fs, li, a, b, win, near)
        rf = refill_flags(fs, li, a, b, win)
        fc.append((sz &amp;gt;= 2).mean())
        fr.append(rf.mean())
        mx.append(sz.max())
        sh.append([(sz[shell_of_event == k] &amp;gt;= 2).mean() if (shell_of_event == k).any() else np.nan for k in range(nsh)])
        shr.append([rf[shell_of_event == k].mean() if (shell_of_event == k).any() else np.nan for k in range(nsh)])
    fc, fr, sh, shr = np.array(fc), np.array(fr), np.array(sh), np.array(shr)
    fo = float((size &amp;gt;= 2).mean())
    ro = float(ref.mean())
    res.update(f_conc=fo, f_conc_ctrl=float(fc.mean()), f_conc_ctrl_sd=float(fc.std(ddof=1)),
               f_conc_binom_se=float(np.sqrt(fo * (1 - fo) / n)),
               refill=ro, refill_ctrl=float(fr.mean()), refill_ctrl_sd=float(fr.std(ddof=1)),
               mean_cluster=float(size[size &amp;gt;= 2].mean()) if (size &amp;gt;= 2).any() else 0.0, max_cluster=int(size.max()), max_cluster_ctrl=float(np.mean(mx)),
               shell_conc=[float((size[shell_of_event == k] &amp;gt;= 2).mean()) if (shell_of_event == k).any() else np.nan
                           for k in range(nsh)],
               shell_conc_ctrl=[float(np.nanmean(sh[:, k])) if np.isfinite(sh[:, k]).any() else np.nan
