Problem it solves: 04 counts a hop whenever the nearest ideal site changes, and its back-hop % only sees a-&amp;gt;b-&amp;gt;a. Near a dopant the
sites fit badly and Li flicker between them, so hop counts there are mostly retracing. Here every Li's hop sequence
(hops_cif/&amp;lt;s&amp;gt;/hops_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv) is analysed as a path on the site graph:
  retrace degree d   hop k completes a palindromic site path of 2d hops (a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a = 1, a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;c&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a = 2, a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a = 2 ...). 0 = none.
  loop erasure       chronological loop erasure of the path: a hop that arrives at ANY site already on the current kept path
                     (within --maxloop kept steps) closes a loop; every hop of that loop is erased and the path is cut back to that
                     site. Palindromes are the loops that retrace themselves; a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;c&amp;gt;a is a loop that does not. Surviving hops = NET.
  small displacement a hop is &amp;quot;small&amp;quot; if the Li's mean position over --win-ps after the hop is &amp;lt; --small A from its mean position
                     over --win-ps before it. A real jump between neighbouring sites moves the Li ~1.5-3 A; flicker between
                     near-degenerate sites near a dopant does not. Hops too close to the start/end of the run cannot be tested (NaN,
                     kept as net).
  ROBUST net hop    NET (not in a loop) AND not small. This removes flicker that loop erasure missed (flicker among 3+ sites that
                    never closes a loop within --maxloop, or loops longer than --maxloop).
Dopant NOT static, loops OWNED BY THE SHELL: shell = Li distance to the dopant in the frame of each hop (dopant position per
frame). Loop erasure / retrace degree are computed separately for every uninterrupted run of one Li's hops in the SAME shell: when
a Li's next hop is in a different shell (for that dopant reference) its path history is cut and a new one starts. A loop that
begins in shell A and would close in shell B is therefore not a loop (both hops are net hops, each owned by its own shell), and
the same loop repeated after the Li entered a new shell is owned by the new shell. The shell is sampled at the hop frames only
(a Li that left and came back to the same shell between two hops keeps its history). Loop statistics now differ per reference
('any', each dopant) because the cuts depend on the reference.
Per distance shell (same shells, far reference and jackknife as 04/05/06; llzo_io):
  all/far     hop rate per Li-time in the shell / same for far Li (all 04 hops)
  NET/far     same, loop-erased hops only
  ROBUST/far  same, net AND not-small hops only
Validation columns: %small|net and %small|loop = % of validated net / erased hops with displacement &amp;lt; --small. Loop hops should be
mostly small; net hops mostly not. (n) = number of hops that could be validated.
Errors: +/- = delete-one-block jackknife over --blocks time blocks of that ONE run (hops of one Li are correlated and the far shell is
not independent of the others: treat differences &amp;lt; 2 SE as noise). * = fewer than 20 hops in that cell (SE unreliable).
Limits: loops longer than --maxloop kept steps are not erased (check 4 and 16); the hop list inherits 04's --min-res and site
assignment; a cell like Ga 0-3 A holds ~1 Li, so its counts per temperature are small - more Li-time (longer runs, more dopants) is the
only cure for that, no analysis can add events.
usage: python 07_hop_loops.py /path/to/MD_run --hops-dir hops_cif/MD_run_1Ga_1Ru [--maxloop 8] [--win-ps 0.3] [--small 1.0]
outputs (in --outdir, default --hops-dir): hop_loops.csv (one row per T, dopant, shell), hop_loops.png, hop_flags_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv (one row
per hop: shell, erased, loop length, retrace degree, displacement, robust flag)
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
import argparse
import csv
import os

import numpy as np

import llzo_io as io

FEW = 20                                         # fewer events than this: open symbol / '*'
MIN_N = getattr(io, 'MIN_N', 5)                  # fewer events than this: no SE (an SE from 0-4 events is meaningless)


def degrees(a, b, dmax):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;a, b: site_from/site_to of ONE Li's hops (time order). deg[k] = largest d &amp;lt;= dmax such that hops k-d+1..k mirror hops
    k-2d+1..k-d (a palindromic path of 2d hops); 0 = no retrace.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = len(a)
    deg = np.zeros(n, int)
    for k in range(1, n):
        for d in range(min(dmax, (k + 1) // 2), 0, -1):
            lo = k - 2 * d + 1
            ok = all(a[k - d + 1 + j] == b[k - d - j] and b[k - d + 1 + j] == a[k - d - j] for j in range(d))
            ok = ok and all(a[i + 1] == b[i] for i in range(lo, k))
            if ok:
                deg[k] = d
                break
    return deg


def loop_erase(a, b, maxloop):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Chronological loop erasure of one Li's site path. Returns (erased (n,) bool, loop_len (n,)): erased = hop belongs to a closed
    loop; loop_len&amp;gt;0 on the hop that closes it (hops in the loop). A break in the path (a[k] != previous b) restarts it.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    n = len(a)
    erased = np.zeros(n, bool)
    loop_len = np.zeros(n, int)
    st = []                                     # kept path: [site, arrival hop]
    for k in range(n):
        if not st or st[-1][0] != a[k]:
            st = [[a[k], -1]]
        q = -1
        for j in range(len(st) - 2, max(-1, len(st) - 2 - maxloop), -1):
            if st[j][0] == b[k]:
                q = j
                break
        if q &amp;gt;= 0:
            for node in st[q + 1:]:
                erased[node[1]] = True
            erased[k] = True
            loop_len[k] = len(st) - q
            del st[q + 1:]
        else:
            st.append([b[k], k])
    return erased, loop_len


def loops_by_shell(L_, f_, sa, sb, cat, maxloop, dmax):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;degrees + loop erasure per (Li, run of consecutive hops in the same shell `cat`). Returns deg, erased, loop_len (n,).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    deg = np.zeros(len(L_), int)
    erased = np.zeros(len(L_), bool)
    llen = np.zeros(len(L_), int)
    order = np.lexsort((f_, L_))
    for grp in np.split(order, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(L_[order])) + 1):
        for g in np.split(grp, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(cat[grp])) + 1):
            deg[g] = degrees(sa[g], sb[g], dmax)
            erased[g], llen[g] = loop_erase(sa[g], sb[g], maxloop)
    return deg, erased, llen


def plot(rows, labs, names, path):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    K = len(names)
    cols = [plt.cm.viridis(k / max(K - 2, 1)) for k in range(K - 1)]
    panels = ((&amp;quot;all_ratio&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;all_ratio_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;all hops / far (as 04)&amp;quot;),
              (&amp;quot;net_ratio&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;net_ratio_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;n_net&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;NET (loops erased) / far&amp;quot;),
              (&amp;quot;rob_ratio&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;rob_ratio_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;n_robust&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;ROBUST (net, not small) / far&amp;quot;))
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(len(labs), 3, figsize=(12.5, 3.2 * len(labs) + 0.9), dpi=150, squeeze=False, sharex=True, sharey=&amp;quot;col&amp;quot;)
    for ri, lab in enumerate(labs):
        for ci, (key, se, nkey, ttl) in enumerate(panels):
            a = axs[ri][ci]
            for k in range(K - 1):
                sel = sorted([r for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;dopant&amp;quot;] == lab and r[&amp;quot;shell&amp;quot;] == names[k]], key=lambda r: r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;])
                if not sel:
                    continue
                x = [r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in sel]
                y = [r[key] for r in sel]
                e = [r[se] if np.isfinite(r[se]) else 0.0 for r in sel]
                a.errorbar(x, y, e, color=cols[k], lw=1.2, capsize=2)
                for r_, y_ in zip(sel, y):
                    a.plot(r_[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;], y_, &amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, ms=4, color=cols[k], mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot; if r_[nkey] &amp;lt; FEW else cols[k])
            a.axhline(1, color=&amp;quot;0.5&amp;quot;, lw=0.8)
            a.set_title(f&amp;quot;{lab}: {ttl}&amp;quot;, fontsize=9)
            if ri == len(labs) - 1:
                a.set_xlabel(&amp;quot;T (K)&amp;quot;)
    h = [Line2D([], [], color=cols[k], marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, lw=2, label=f&amp;quot;Li {names[k]} from dopant&amp;quot;) for k in range(K - 1)]
    h.append(Line2D([], [], color=&amp;quot;0.4&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, lw=0, label=f&amp;quot;open: &amp;lt; {FEW} hops&amp;quot;))
    fig.legend(handles=h, loc=&amp;quot;lower center&amp;quot;, ncol=3, fontsize=8, frameon=False)
    fig.suptitle(&amp;quot;hop rate relative to far Li, per temperature; error bars = block jackknife within each run&amp;quot;, fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.08, 1, 0.97))
    fig.savefig(path)
    plt.close(fig)


def _ratio(v, se, n, ref):
    if ref:
        return f&amp;quot;{'1 (ref)':&amp;gt;13} &amp;quot;
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return f&amp;quot;{'n/a':&amp;gt;13} &amp;quot;
    s = f&amp;quot;{v:5.2f}+/-{se:4.2f}&amp;quot; if np.isfinite(se) else f&amp;quot;{v:5.2f}+/- nan&amp;quot;
    return f&amp;quot;{s}{'*' if n &amp;lt; FEW else ' '}&amp;quot;


def _small(p, n):
    return f&amp;quot;{p:5.1f} ({n:3d})&amp;quot; if n else f&amp;quot;{'n/a':&amp;gt;10}&amp;quot;


def print_T(T, rows, a_):
    print(f&amp;quot;\nT = {T} K   maxloop {a_.maxloop}; small = displacement &amp;lt; {a_.small} A over {a_.win_ps} ps; +/- = block jackknife SE; &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;* = &amp;lt; {FEW} hops&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;{'dopant':&amp;gt;6} {'shell':&amp;gt;6} {'hops':&amp;gt;6} {'%loop':&amp;gt;6} {'%palin':&amp;gt;6} {'%nonpal':&amp;gt;7} {'net':&amp;gt;5} {'robust':&amp;gt;6} &amp;quot;
          f&amp;quot;{'all/far':&amp;gt;14} {'NET/far':&amp;gt;14} {'ROBUST/far':&amp;gt;14} {'%small|net (n)':&amp;gt;14} {'%small|loop (n)':&amp;gt;15}&amp;quot;)
    for r in rows:
        if not r[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;]:
            continue
        ref = r[&amp;quot;is_far&amp;quot;]
        print(f&amp;quot;{r['dopant']:&amp;gt;6} {r['shell']:&amp;gt;6} {r['n_hops']:6d} {r['pct_in_loops']:6.1f} {r['pct_palindromic']:6.1f} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{r['pct_nonpal']:7.1f} {r['n_net']:5d} {r['n_robust']:6d} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{_ratio(r['all_ratio'], r['all_ratio_se'], r['n_hops'], ref):&amp;gt;14} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{_ratio(r['net_ratio'], r['net_ratio_se'], r['n_net'], ref):&amp;gt;14} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{_ratio(r['rob_ratio'], r['rob_ratio_se'], r['n_robust'], ref):&amp;gt;14} &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{_small(r['pct_small_net'], r['n_val_net']):&amp;gt;14} {_small(r['pct_small_loop'], r['n_val_loop']):&amp;gt;15}&amp;quot;)


def definitions(a_, names):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Full legend of every quantity in the tables, printed at the end of the log (and saved as hop_loops_definitions.txt).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    sh = &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(names)
    return f&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
{'=' * 118}
DEFINITIONS (this run: maxloop {a_.maxloop}, dmax {a_.dmax}, small &amp;lt; {a_.small} A, window {a_.win_ps} ps, {a_.blocks} jackknife blocks, shells {sh})
{'=' * 118}
INPUT
  A hop = one row of hops_cif/&amp;lt;system&amp;gt;/hops_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv (written by 04): Li number, frame, site_from, site_to. 04 declares a hop when a Li's
  nearest ideal crystal site changes (and the Li stays on the new site for 04's --min-res). Each temperature is a separate, independent
  MD run, so everything below is computed and reported per temperature; nothing is pooled over T.
  Each Li's hops are put in time order and treated as a PATH on the graph of sites: site_from -&amp;gt; site_to -&amp;gt; ...
  The dopant moves (its position is taken per frame) and loops are OWNED BY THE SHELL: the path of a Li is cut whenever its next hop is
  in a different shell (for the dopant of that row), so a loop never spans two shells and a repeat in a new shell belongs to the new shell.

SHELL (rows)  and  DOPANT (rows)
  shell   distance of the Li to the dopant IN THE FRAME OF THE HOP ({sh}). The Li's own position at the hop decides the shell, not the
          site it leaves.  &amp;gt;{a_.shells[-1]:g}A is the FAR reference: Li that are not near the dopant. Every ratio is relative to it.
  dopant  the atom the distance is measured from. Ga1, Ru1 ... are individual dopant atoms; 'any' uses whichever dopant is nearest to the Li.
          The far row is the same Li pool for every dopant (identical numbers in all blocks). With far=all a Li that is far from THIS
          dopant but near ANOTHER one is excluded from both near and far (it is neither).

PATH QUANTITIES (per hop)
  retrace degree d  hop k completes an exact palindrome of 2d hops: a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a is d=1, a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;c&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a is d=2, a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a is d=2. 0 = no retrace.
                    Checked up to --dmax. A palindrome is an exact return along the same sites.
  loop erasure      chronological. Keep a current path for each Li. A hop that arrives at ANY site already on the current path (looking back at
                    most --maxloop kept steps) closes a LOOP. Every hop of that loop is ERASED and the path is cut back to that site.
                    It is not limited to returning to the start: 1&amp;gt;2, 2&amp;gt;3, 3&amp;gt;4, 4&amp;gt;2 closes the loop 2&amp;gt;3&amp;gt;4&amp;gt;2 (3 hops erased); 1&amp;gt;2 is kept.
                    a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;a is the shortest loop; a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;c&amp;gt;a is a loop that is not a palindrome. A hop that does not start where the previous
                    one ended (a gap in the path) restarts the path. Loops longer than --maxloop are NOT erased.
  ERASED / in loop  the hop belongs to a closed loop. Erased does not mean fake: a real jump that is later undone is erased too. It only means
                    the hop contributed no lasting progress along the path.
  NET hop           a hop that survives loop erasure (not in any closed loop): progress along a self-avoiding path.
  small hop         the Li's mean position over {a_.win_ps} ps AFTER the hop is &amp;lt; {a_.small} A from its mean position over {a_.win_ps} ps BEFORE it. A real
                    jump between neighbouring sites moves the Li about 1.5-3 A; flicker between near-degenerate sites (typical close to a
                    dopant, where the sites fit badly) barely moves the mean position. Hops within {a_.win_ps} ps of the start or end of the
                    run cannot be tested (displacement = nan): they are kept as non-small and counted under 'not testable'.
  ROBUST net hop    NET and not small. Removes flicker that loop erasure misses (flicker among 3 or more sites that never closes a loop
                    within --maxloop, and loops longer than --maxloop).

COLUMNS OF THE PER-TEMPERATURE TABLES
  hops          all hops of 04 in that shell at this T.
  %loop         100 * (erased hops) / hops. Both the outward and the return hop of a loop count, so %loop &amp;gt; %palin.
  %palin        100 * (hops with retrace degree &amp;gt;= 1) / hops: exact retraces only.
  %nonpal       100 * (hops that close a loop but are not exact retraces) / hops, e.g. a&amp;gt;b&amp;gt;c&amp;gt;a. Counted on the closing hop only, so small.
  net           number of hops that are not erased (NET hops).
  robust        number of ROBUST net hops (net and not small).
  all/far       (hops in the shell / Li-time in the shell) / (same for the far shell). Li-time = time all Li spent in that shell, so a shell
                holding few Li is not penalised: this is a hop RATE per Li, not a raw count. 1 = same as far Li, &amp;gt;1 = more hops, &amp;lt;1 = fewer.
                Counts all 04 hops, loops included.
  NET/far       the same ratio using NET hops only: how often a Li in this shell makes progress that is not undone, vs far Li.
  ROBUST/far    the same ratio using ROBUST hops only: NET hops that also moved the Li by &amp;gt;= {a_.small} A.
  Reading all/far with NET/far: high all/far with low NET/far = lots of hopping that is undone (retracing / flicker); both near 1 = same
                transport as far Li; both low = genuinely slowed. NET/far and ROBUST/far are the transport-relevant ones; compare them with
                the 05 MSD ratio and the 06 non-back rate. If ROBUST/far is much below NET/far, the net hops in that shell were mostly flicker.
  +/-           delete-one-block jackknife SE over {a_.blocks} time blocks of that single run (a hop list of one Li is correlated, and the far
                shell is not independent of the others): treat differences smaller than 2 SE as noise. With only {a_.blocks} blocks the SE is itself
                uncertain (~35 %). Blank (nan) when the cell has fewer than {MIN_N} events: an SE from 0-4 events is meaningless.
  *             fewer than {FEW} events in that cell (hops for all/far, net hops for NET/far, robust hops for ROBUST/far): the value is
                not reliable. Open symbols in the plot mean the same.
  1 (ref)       the far shell, equal to 1 by definition.
  %small|net (n)   % of the NET hops whose displacement is &amp;lt; {a_.small} A, among the n net hops that could be tested.
  %small|loop (n)  % of the ERASED hops whose displacement is &amp;lt; {a_.small} A, among the n erased hops that could be tested.
                   If erasure separates flicker from real jumps, %small|loop should be high and %small|net low. A high %small|net means net
                   hops are still partly flicker (that is why ROBUST exists). Only hops that could be tested are counted, so n &amp;lt; hops.

THE T=... SUMMARY LINE
  hops = all hops at that T; in loops = erased; palindromic retraces = retrace degree &amp;gt;= 1; net = not erased; robust net = net and
  displacement &amp;gt;= {a_.small} A; not testable = hops too close to the ends of the run for the displacement test.

LIMITS
  Loops longer than --maxloop are not erased (run --maxloop 4 and 16). The 'small' threshold is a judgement (run --small 0.7 and 1.5).
  The hop list inherits 04's --min-res and its site assignment, which fits badly right next to a dopant. A cell such as Ga 0-3 A holds about
  one Li, so its counts per temperature are small; more Li-time (longer runs, more dopants) is the only way to add events. Look for the
  same sign at all temperatures rather than one pooled number. Loop erasure works on site indices, so a genuine path that winds around the
  periodic box and returns to the same site index would also be erased (only non-palindromic loops are affected, a few % of hops).

FILES
  hop_loops.csv           one row per (T, dopant, shell): n_hops, n_loop, n_net, n_robust, pct_in_loops(+_se), pct_palindromic, pct_nonpal,
                          all_/net_/rob_ rate (per Li per ps), ratio and ratio_se, pct_small_net, n_val_net, pct_small_loop, n_val_loop,
                          n_not_testable, maxloop, small_A, win_ps.
  hop_loops.png           all / NET / ROBUST ratio vs T, one line per shell; open symbols = fewer than {FEW} events.
  hop_flags_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv     one row per hop: li, frame, site_from, site_to, shell_any, d_any_A (distance to nearest dopant), in_loop, loop_len
                          (hops in the loop, on the closing hop), retrace_deg, disp_A, small, robust_net (1/0).
{'=' * 118}
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;target&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--hops-dir&amp;quot;, required=True)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--T&amp;quot;, type=int, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--tmin&amp;quot;, type=float)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--maxloop&amp;quot;, type=int, default=8, help=&amp;quot;longest loop (kept steps) that is erased&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dmax&amp;quot;, type=int, default=8, help=&amp;quot;longest palindrome (degree) checked&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--win-ps&amp;quot;, type=float, default=0.3, help=&amp;quot;displacement window for the small-hop test&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--small&amp;quot;, type=float, default=1.0, help=&amp;quot;hops with displacement below this (A) are 'small' (flicker)&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--shells&amp;quot;, type=float, nargs=&amp;quot;*&amp;quot;, default=list(io.DEFAULT_SHELLS))
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--far&amp;quot;, choices=[&amp;quot;all&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;self&amp;quot;], default=&amp;quot;all&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--blocks&amp;quot;, type=int, default=io.DEFAULT_BLOCKS)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--no-flags&amp;quot;, action=&amp;quot;store_true&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;do not write the per-hop hop_flags_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv files&amp;quot;)
    ap.add_argument(&amp;quot;--outdir&amp;quot;)
    io.add_config_args(ap)
    a_ = ap.parse_args()

    cfg = io.load_config(a_.target, None, a_)
    temps = a_.T or io.temperatures(cfg)
    outdir = a_.outdir or a_.hops_dir
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    edges = np.array(a_.shells, float)
    names = io.shell_names(edges)
    K = len(names)
    rows = []
    for T in temps:
        p = os.path.join(a_.hops_dir, f&amp;quot;hops_T{T}K.csv&amp;quot;)
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            print(&amp;quot;missing&amp;quot;, p)
            continue
        traj = io.prepare(cfg, io.dump_path(cfg, T), a_.tmin, verbose=False)
        drift = io.framework_drift(cfg, traj)
        li = traj.pos[:, io.li_mask(cfg, traj), :] - drift[:, None, :]
        F, N = li.shape[:2]
        dt = float(np.median(np.diff(traj.time_ps)))
        nwin = max(1, int(round(a_.win_ps / dt)))
        dops, cnt = {}, {}
        for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]:
            for i in np.where(traj.types == t)[0]:
                el = cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;][t]
                cnt[el] = cnt.get(el, 0) + 1
                dops[f&amp;quot;{el}{cnt[el]}&amp;quot;] = io.dopant_xyz(cfg, traj, drift, i)      # smoothed over --dop-smooth-ps
        if not dops:
            raise SystemExit(&amp;quot;no dopant atoms: nothing to compare&amp;quot;)
        dists = {lab: io.dist_to(li, x, traj.L) for lab, x in dops.items()}
        dnear = np.min(list(dists.values()), axis=0)
        dists = {&amp;quot;any&amp;quot;: dnear, **dists}

        H = list(csv.DictReader(open(p)))
        if not H:
            print(f&amp;quot;T={T}: no hops in {p}&amp;quot;)
            continue
        L_ = np.array([int(h[&amp;quot;li&amp;quot;]) for h in H])
        f_ = np.array([int(h[&amp;quot;frame&amp;quot;]) for h in H])
        sa = np.array([int(h[&amp;quot;site_from&amp;quot;]) for h in H])
        sb = np.array([int(h[&amp;quot;site_to&amp;quot;]) for h in H])
        # loops are owned by the shell: history cut when the Li's shell changes (reference 'any' here = the summary line and
        # hop_flags csv; every dopant reference gets its own loops below)
        cat_any = io.shell_labels(dnear, dnear, edges, a_.far)[f_, L_]
        deg, erased, llen = loops_by_shell(L_, f_, sa, sb, cat_any, a_.maxloop, a_.dmax)
        disp = np.full(len(H), np.nan)
        for n, (i, f) in enumerate(zip(L_, f_)):
            if f - nwin &amp;gt;= 0 and f + nwin + 1 &amp;lt;= F:
                disp[n] = np.linalg.norm(li[f + 1:f + nwin + 1, i].mean(0) - li[f - nwin:f, i].mean(0))
        valid = np.isfinite(disp)
        small = valid &amp;amp; (disp &amp;lt; a_.small)
        flicker = erased | small                 # not a robust net hop
        nonpal = (llen &amp;gt; 0) &amp;amp; (deg == 0)
        print(f&amp;quot;T={T}: {len(H)} hops, {int(erased.sum())} in loops ({100 * erased.mean():.0f} %), &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{int((deg &amp;gt; 0).sum())} palindromic retraces, {int((~erased).sum())} net, &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;{int((~flicker).sum())} robust net (net and displacement &amp;gt;= {a_.small} A; {int((~valid).sum())} hops not testable)&amp;quot;)

        rows_T = []
        shell_any = None
        for lab, d_ in dists.items():
            sh = io.shell_labels(d_, dnear, edges, a_.far)
            C = io.block_counts(sh, K, a_.blocks)
            cat = sh[f_, L_]
            if lab == &amp;quot;any&amp;quot;:
                shell_any = cat
                deg_l, erased_l, llen_l = deg, erased, llen
            else:
                deg_l, erased_l, llen_l = loops_by_shell(L_, f_, sa, sb, cat, a_.maxloop, a_.dmax)
            flicker_l = erased_l | small
            nonpal_l = (llen_l &amp;gt; 0) &amp;amp; (deg_l == 0)
            kw = dict(litime_blk=C * dt, nmean=C.sum(0) / F, F=F, nblocks=a_.blocks)
            ones = np.ones(len(H), bool)
            r_loop = io.hop_shell_rows(T, lab, &amp;quot;loops&amp;quot;, names, cat, f_, erased_l, ones, **kw)
            r_pal = io.hop_shell_rows(T, lab, &amp;quot;palin&amp;quot;, names, cat, f_, deg_l &amp;gt; 0, ones, **kw)
            r_rob = io.hop_shell_rows(T, lab, &amp;quot;robust&amp;quot;, names, cat, f_, flicker_l, ones, **kw)
            for kk in range(K):
                m = cat == kk
                a1, a2, a3 = r_loop[kk], r_pal[kk], r_rob[kk]
                mn, ml = m &amp;amp; ~erased_l &amp;amp; valid, m &amp;amp; erased_l &amp;amp; valid
                n_net_, n_rob_ = a1[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] - a1[&amp;quot;n_back&amp;quot;], a3[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] - a3[&amp;quot;n_back&amp;quot;]
                nse = a1[&amp;quot;nonback_ratio_se&amp;quot;] if n_net_ &amp;gt;= MIN_N else np.nan
                rse = a3[&amp;quot;nonback_ratio_se&amp;quot;] if n_rob_ &amp;gt;= MIN_N else np.nan
                rows_T.append(dict(
                    T=T, dopant=lab, shell=names[kk], is_far=(kk == K - 1), n_Li_mean=a1[&amp;quot;n_Li_mean&amp;quot;], n_hops=a1[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;],
                    n_loop=a1[&amp;quot;n_back&amp;quot;], n_net=a1[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] - a1[&amp;quot;n_back&amp;quot;], n_robust=a3[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] - a3[&amp;quot;n_back&amp;quot;],
                    pct_in_loops=a1[&amp;quot;back_pct&amp;quot;], pct_in_loops_se=a1[&amp;quot;back_pct_se&amp;quot;], pct_palindromic=a2[&amp;quot;back_pct&amp;quot;],
                    pct_nonpal=100 * nonpal_l[m].mean() if m.any() else np.nan,
                    all_rate=a1[&amp;quot;rate_per_Li_ps&amp;quot;], all_ratio=a1[&amp;quot;rate_ratio_to_far&amp;quot;], all_ratio_se=a1[&amp;quot;rate_ratio_se&amp;quot;],
                    net_rate=a1[&amp;quot;nonback_rate_per_Li_ps&amp;quot;], net_ratio=a1[&amp;quot;nonback_ratio_to_far&amp;quot;], net_ratio_se=nse,
                    rob_rate=a3[&amp;quot;nonback_rate_per_Li_ps&amp;quot;], rob_ratio=a3[&amp;quot;nonback_ratio_to_far&amp;quot;], rob_ratio_se=rse,
                    pct_small_net=100 * small[mn].mean() if mn.any() else np.nan, n_val_net=int(mn.sum()),
                    pct_small_loop=100 * small[ml].mean() if ml.any() else np.nan, n_val_loop=int(ml.sum()),
                    n_not_testable=int((m &amp;amp; ~valid).sum()), maxloop=a_.maxloop, small_A=a_.small, win_ps=a_.win_ps))
        rows += rows_T
        print_T(T, [r for r in rows_T], a_)

        if not a_.no_flags:
            with open(os.path.join(outdir, f&amp;quot;hop_flags_T{T}K.csv&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;, newline=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;) as fh:
                w = csv.writer(fh)
                w.writerow([&amp;quot;li&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;frame&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;site_from&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;site_to&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;shell_any&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;d_any_A&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;in_loop&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;loop_len&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;retrace_deg&amp;quot;,
                            &amp;quot;disp_A&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;small&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;robust_net&amp;quot;])
                for n in np.argsort(f_, kind=&amp;quot;stable&amp;quot;):
                    w.writerow([L_[n], f_[n], sa[n], sb[n], names[shell_any[n]] if shell_any[n] &amp;gt;= 0 else &amp;quot;excluded&amp;quot;,
                                f&amp;quot;{dnear[f_[n], L_[n]]:.3f}&amp;quot;, int(erased[n]), llen[n], deg[n],
                                f&amp;quot;{disp[n]:.3f}&amp;quot; if valid[n] else &amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;, int(small[n]), int(not flicker[n])])

    if not rows:
        raise SystemExit(&amp;quot;no hops read&amp;quot;)
    labs = list(dict.fromkeys(r[&amp;quot;dopant&amp;quot;] for r in rows))
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, &amp;quot;hop_loops.csv&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;, newline=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;) as fh:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    plot(rows, labs, names, os.path.join(outdir, &amp;quot;hop_loops.png&amp;quot;))
    print(f&amp;quot;\n[+] {outdir}/hop_loops.csv hop_loops.png&amp;quot; + (&amp;quot;&amp;quot; if a_.no_flags else &amp;quot; hop_flags_T&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.csv&amp;quot;))
    txt = definitions(a_, names)
    print(txt)
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, &amp;quot;hop_loops_definitions.txt&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;) as fh:
        fh.write(txt)
    print(&amp;quot;read: NET/far and ROBUST/far are the transport-relevant ratios; each T is an independent run, so look for the same sign &amp;quot;
          &amp;quot;at all T. Full definitions are above and in hop_loops_definitions.txt.&amp;quot;)

if __name__ == &amp;quot;__main__&amp;quot;:
    main()
&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/details&gt;&lt;details class=&quot;attachment&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Attachment: llzo_io.py&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
llzo_io.py - shared helpers for the doped-LLZO MD analysis scripts.

Expects a LAMMPS dump written with:
    dump 1 all custom 100 &amp;lt;file&amp;gt; id type xu yu zu
(orthogonal box, unwrapped coordinates, all atoms).

Everything composition-specific (type -&amp;gt; element map, dopant types, run length,
analysis window ...) lives in a per-system config file (YAML or JSON), so the
same scripts work for every CIF/dopant set.
&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
from __future__ import annotations

import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import warnings
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

import llzo_svg  # noqa: F401  (every saved .png also gets an editable .svg)

KB_EV = 8.617333262e-5  # eV/K
TILT_TOL = 0.05        # A: triclinic tilt is always ignored; a warning says 'LARGE' above this

MASSES = {  # amu; extend if you use other dopants
    &amp;quot;Li&amp;quot;: 6.94, &amp;quot;La&amp;quot;: 138.905, &amp;quot;Zr&amp;quot;: 91.224, &amp;quot;O&amp;quot;: 15.999, &amp;quot;Ga&amp;quot;: 69.723,
    &amp;quot;Ru&amp;quot;: 101.07, &amp;quot;Al&amp;quot;: 26.982, &amp;quot;Ta&amp;quot;: 180.948, &amp;quot;Nb&amp;quot;: 92.906, &amp;quot;Ca&amp;quot;: 40.078,
    &amp;quot;Ba&amp;quot;: 137.327, &amp;quot;Sr&amp;quot;: 87.62, &amp;quot;Y&amp;quot;: 88.906, &amp;quot;W&amp;quot;: 183.84, &amp;quot;Mo&amp;quot;: 95.95,
    &amp;quot;Sb&amp;quot;: 121.76, &amp;quot;Bi&amp;quot;: 208.98, &amp;quot;Ge&amp;quot;: 72.63, &amp;quot;Sn&amp;quot;: 118.71, &amp;quot;Ti&amp;quot;: 47.867,
    &amp;quot;Hf&amp;quot;: 178.49, &amp;quot;Fe&amp;quot;: 55.845, &amp;quot;Sc&amp;quot;: 44.956, &amp;quot;In&amp;quot;: 114.818, &amp;quot;Mg&amp;quot;: 24.305,
    &amp;quot;Zn&amp;quot;: 65.38, &amp;quot;Te&amp;quot;: 127.60, &amp;quot;Ce&amp;quot;: 140.116, &amp;quot;Si&amp;quot;: 28.085, &amp;quot;V&amp;quot;: 50.942,&amp;quot;Nd&amp;quot;:144.242,&amp;quot;Tc&amp;quot;:98.9062,&amp;quot;Co&amp;quot;:58.933194,
}

BASE_ELEMENTS = {&amp;quot;Li&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;La&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;Zr&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;O&amp;quot;}   # anything else in element_list is treated as a dopant

DEFAULTS = {
    &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;: None,             # None -&amp;gt; name of the folder holding the dumps
    &amp;quot;prefix&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;lmp&amp;quot;,
    &amp;quot;dump_pattern&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;dump_{prefix}_{T}K.lammpstrj&amp;quot;,
    &amp;quot;temperatures&amp;quot;: None,     # None -&amp;gt; detected from the dump files in the folder
    &amp;quot;unit_cell_a&amp;quot;: 12.9827,   # conventional cubic LLZO cell edge, Angstrom
    &amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;: None,             # e.g. [1, 1, 2]; None -&amp;gt; derived from the dump box
    &amp;quot;dt_ps&amp;quot;: 0.001,           # MD timestep in ps
    &amp;quot;run_steps&amp;quot;: None,        # MD steps of the run (excludes minimisation); see prepare()
    &amp;quot;tmin_ps&amp;quot;: 100.0,         # analysis window starts here (ps after run start)
    &amp;quot;types&amp;quot;: None,            # None -&amp;gt; read from &amp;lt;folder&amp;gt;/element_list
    &amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;: None,     # None -&amp;gt; every element not in BASE_ELEMENTS
    &amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;: 1.0,     # dopant position used for SHELLS = centred moving average over this many ps (0 = instantaneous)
}

SETTINGS_NAME = &amp;quot;analysis_defaults.yaml&amp;quot;


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# config
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _read_settings(path: str) -&amp;gt; dict:
    with open(path) as f:
        text = f.read()
    if path.lower().endswith(&amp;quot;.json&amp;quot;):
        return json.loads(text) or {}
    try:
        import yaml
    except ImportError:
        sys.exit(&amp;quot;PyYAML is required for .yaml settings (pip install pyyaml), or use a .json file.&amp;quot;)
    return yaml.safe_load(text) or {}


def _find_settings(folder: str | None, explicit: str | None) -&amp;gt; str | None:
    if explicit:
        if not os.path.exists(explicit):
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;settings file not found: {explicit}&amp;quot;)
        return explicit
    cands = []
    if folder:
        cands.append(os.path.join(folder, SETTINGS_NAME))
    cands += [os.path.join(os.getcwd(), SETTINGS_NAME),
              os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), SETTINGS_NAME)]
    for c in cands:
        if os.path.exists(c):
            return c
    return None


def read_element_list(folder: str, explicit: str | None = None, also: str | None = None) -&amp;gt; list[str]:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Elements for LAMMPS atom types 1..N, in order, e.g. 'Li La Y Zr Ta O'.
    Order of search: explicit file (--element-list), then &amp;lt;folder&amp;gt;/element_list[.txt], then &amp;lt;also&amp;gt;/ (target folder).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    cands = [explicit] if explicit else []
    for d in dict.fromkeys([folder, also or folder]):
        cands += [os.path.join(d, n) for n in (&amp;quot;element_list&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;element_list.txt&amp;quot;)]
    for p in cands:
        if os.path.exists(p):
            with open(p) as f:
                els = f.read().replace(&amp;quot;,&amp;quot;, &amp;quot; &amp;quot;).split()
            if not els:
                sys.exit(f&amp;quot;{p} is empty&amp;quot;)
            return els
    sys.exit(f&amp;quot;No 'element_list' file in {folder}. Create one containing the elements in atom-type &amp;quot;
             f&amp;quot;order, e.g.  Li La Zr Ga Ru O   (or pass --elements / --element-list, or put a 'types' map in the settings file).&amp;quot;)


def add_config_args(ap) -&amp;gt; None:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;System-specific inputs as flags; each overrides the settings file / element_list / defaults.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    g = ap.add_argument_group(&amp;quot;system layout (override settings file)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dump-dir&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;folder with the dumps (absolute, or relative to the target folder; default: the target folder)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--prefix&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;dump file prefix in dump_{prefix}_{T}K.lammpstrj&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dump-pattern&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;dump file name pattern with {prefix} and {T}, e.g. 'dump_{prefix}_{T}K.lammpstrj'&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--elements&amp;quot;, nargs=&amp;quot;+&amp;quot;, metavar=&amp;quot;EL&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;elements for atom types 1..N in order, e.g. Li La Zr Ga Ru O (replaces element_list)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--element-list&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;path to an element_list file (absolute, or relative to the target folder)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dopants&amp;quot;, nargs=&amp;quot;+&amp;quot;, metavar=&amp;quot;EL&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;dopant elements, e.g. Ga Ru (default: every element not Li La Zr O)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--fold&amp;quot;, type=int, nargs=3, metavar=(&amp;quot;NX&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;NY&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;NZ&amp;quot;), help=&amp;quot;supercell: conventional cells along a b c, e.g. 1 1 2 (default: from box / unit_cell_a)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--name&amp;quot;, help=&amp;quot;system name used for results/&amp;lt;name&amp;gt; (default: target folder name)&amp;quot;)
    g.add_argument(&amp;quot;--dop-smooth-ps&amp;quot;, type=float, metavar=&amp;quot;PS&amp;quot;,
                   help=&amp;quot;shell analysis: dopant position = centred moving average over PS ps, so dopant vibration does not move the shell &amp;quot;
                        &amp;quot;edges and a Li at an edge does not flip shell (default 1.0; 0 = instantaneous; a static dopant is unaffected)&amp;quot;)


def load_config(target: str, settings: str | None = None, args=None) -&amp;gt; dict:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;target = folder with the dumps (+ element_list)  OR  a settings .yaml/.json whose
    dump_dir points at that folder. Shared numbers (run_steps, tmin_ps, ...) come from
    analysis_defaults.yaml (found in the folder, the current directory, or next to the scripts)
    or from `settings`.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    target = os.path.abspath(target)
    cfg = dict(DEFAULTS)
    if os.path.isdir(target):
        folder = target
        spath = _find_settings(folder, settings)
        if spath:
            cfg.update(_read_settings(spath))
        cfg[&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;] = os.path.basename(folder.rstrip(os.sep))      # folder name is the system name
    else:
        spath = target
        cfg.update(_read_settings(spath))
        d = cfg.get(&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;) or &amp;quot;.&amp;quot;
        folder = d if os.path.isabs(d) else os.path.join(os.path.dirname(spath), d)
        folder = os.path.abspath(folder)
        if not cfg.get(&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;):
            cfg[&amp;quot;name&amp;quot;] = os.path.basename(folder.rstrip(os.sep))
    base = folder                                                  # target folder (or the settings file's dump_dir)
    g = lambda k: getattr(args, k, None) if args is not None else None
    if g(&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;):
        folder = os.path.abspath(g(&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;) if os.path.isabs(g(&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;)) else os.path.join(base, g(&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;)))
    for k in (&amp;quot;prefix&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;dump_pattern&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;):
        if g(k):
            cfg[k] = g(k)
    if g(&amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;):
        cfg[&amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;] = list(g(&amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;))
    if g(&amp;quot;elements&amp;quot;):
        cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;] = {i + 1: el for i, el in enumerate(g(&amp;quot;elements&amp;quot;))}
        if not g(&amp;quot;dopants&amp;quot;):
            cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;] = None                             # old dopant_types refer to the old type map
    if g(&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;) is not None:
        cfg[&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;] = float(g(&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;))
    cfg[&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;] = max(0.0, float(cfg.get(&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;) or 0.0))
    cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;] = folder
    cfg[&amp;quot;_settings_file&amp;quot;] = spath

    if cfg.get(&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;):
        cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;] = {int(k): str(v) for k, v in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items()}
    else:
        ef = g(&amp;quot;element_list&amp;quot;)
        if ef and not os.path.isabs(ef):
            ef = os.path.join(base, ef)
        els = read_element_list(folder, ef, base)
        cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;] = {i + 1: el for i, el in enumerate(els)}
    if g(&amp;quot;dopants&amp;quot;):
        cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;] = [t for t, el in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items() if el in g(&amp;quot;dopants&amp;quot;)]
        missing = [e for e in g(&amp;quot;dopants&amp;quot;) if e not in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].values()]
        if missing:
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;--dopants {missing} not in the element list {list(cfg['types'].values())}&amp;quot;)
    if cfg.get(&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;) is None:
        cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;] = [t for t, el in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items() if el not in BASE_ELEMENTS]
    cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;] = [int(t) for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]]

    for t, el in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items():
        if el not in MASSES:
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;Element '{el}' (type {t}) missing from MASSES in llzo_io.py - add it.&amp;quot;)
    for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]:
        if t not in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;]:
            sys.exit(f&amp;quot;dopant type {t} is not in 'types'&amp;quot;)
    if not li_types(cfg):
        sys.exit(&amp;quot;no type mapped to element 'Li' (check element_list)&amp;quot;)

    tmap = &amp;quot; &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{t}={el}&amp;quot; for t, el in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items())
    dop = &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{cfg['types'][t]}(type {t})&amp;quot; for t in cfg[&amp;quot;dopant_types&amp;quot;]) or &amp;quot;none&amp;quot;
    print(f&amp;quot;[config] system '{cfg['name']}'  types: {tmap}  dopants: {dop}  dopant smoothing: {cfg['dop_smooth_ps']:g} ps&amp;quot;)
    return cfg


def li_types(cfg: dict) -&amp;gt; list[int]:
    return [t for t, el in cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;].items() if el == &amp;quot;Li&amp;quot;]


def temperatures(cfg: dict) -&amp;gt; list[int]:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Temperatures from the config, or detected from dump_*_&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.lammpstrj files in the folder.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    if cfg.get(&amp;quot;temperatures&amp;quot;):
        return [int(t) for t in cfg[&amp;quot;temperatures&amp;quot;]]
    found = set()
    pat = cfg[&amp;quot;dump_pattern&amp;quot;]
    if &amp;quot;{T}&amp;quot; in pat:                      # temperatures from the dump pattern itself (e.g. 'MD-{T}/dump.{T}.lammpstrj')
        parts = [x.format(prefix=cfg[&amp;quot;prefix&amp;quot;]) for x in pat.split(&amp;quot;{T}&amp;quot;)]
        rx = re.compile(re.escape(parts[0]) + &amp;quot;(\\d+)&amp;quot; + re.escape(parts[1]) + &amp;quot;&amp;quot;.join(&amp;quot;\\1&amp;quot; + re.escape(x) for x in parts[2:]))
        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;], &amp;quot;*&amp;quot;.join(parts))):
            m = rx.fullmatch(os.path.relpath(p, cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;]))
            if m:
                found.add(int(m.group(1)))
    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;], &amp;quot;*.lammpstrj&amp;quot;)):
        m = re.search(r&amp;quot;_(\d+)K\.lammpstrj$&amp;quot;, os.path.basename(p))
        if m:
            found.add(int(m.group(1)))
    if not found:
        sys.exit(f&amp;quot;no dumps found in {cfg['dump_dir']} (pattern {pat!r}, or *_&amp;lt;T&amp;gt;K.lammpstrj)&amp;quot;)
    return sorted(found)


def dump_path(cfg: dict, T) -&amp;gt; str:
    p = os.path.join(cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;], cfg[&amp;quot;dump_pattern&amp;quot;].format(prefix=cfg[&amp;quot;prefix&amp;quot;], T=T))
    if os.path.exists(p):
        return p
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(cfg[&amp;quot;dump_dir&amp;quot;], f&amp;quot;*_{T}K.lammpstrj&amp;quot;))
    if len(hits) == 1:
        return hits[0]
    if len(hits) &amp;gt; 1:
        sys.exit(f&amp;quot;several dumps match T={T} K in {cfg['dump_dir']}: {sorted(os.path.basename(h) for h in hits)}; &amp;quot;
                 f&amp;quot;set 'prefix' or 'dump_pattern' in the settings file&amp;quot;)
    sys.exit(f&amp;quot;dump for T={T} K not found (looked for {p} and *_{T}K.lammpstrj)&amp;quot;)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# dump reading
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class Trajectory:
    steps: np.ndarray      # (F,)   LAMMPS timestep of each frame
    time_ps: np.ndarray    # (F,)   time since the start of the MD run
    lo: np.ndarray         # (3,)   box lower bound (frame 0)
    L: np.ndarray          # (F,3)  box lengths, Angstrom
    types: np.ndarray      # (N,)   atom types, sorted by atom id
    ids: np.ndarray        # (N,)
    pos: np.ndarray        # (F,N,3) unwrapped positions, Angstrom


def read_dump(path: str) -&amp;gt; Trajectory:
    steps, Ls, frames = [], [], []
    types = ids0 = lo0 = None
    tri_max = -1.0                       # largest |tilt| seen (triclinic dumps only)
    with open(path) as f:
        while True:
            line = f.readline()
            if not line:
                break
            if not line.startswith(&amp;quot;ITEM: TIMESTEP&amp;quot;):
                raise ValueError(f&amp;quot;{path}: expected 'ITEM: TIMESTEP', got {line[:40]!r}&amp;quot;)
            step = int(f.readline())
            f.readline()                      # ITEM: NUMBER OF ATOMS
            n = int(f.readline())
            hdr = f.readline()                # ITEM: BOX BOUNDS ...
            b = [f.readline().split() for _ in range(3)]
            lo = np.array([float(x[0]) for x in b])
            hi = np.array([float(x[1]) for x in b])
            if &amp;quot;xy&amp;quot; in hdr:                   # triclinic header: 3rd column = tilt xy xz yz. Tilt is IGNORED (box treated as orthogonal)
                xy, xz, yz = tilt = [float(x[2]) for x in b]
                tri_max = max(tri_max, max(abs(t) for t in tilt))
                # dump bounds are the bounding box of the tilted cell: remove the tilt extent to get the true box edges
                ex = [min(0.0, xy, xz, xy + xz), max(0.0, xy, xz, xy + xz)]
                ey = [min(0.0, yz), max(0.0, yz)]
                lo = lo - np.array([ex[0], ey[0], 0.0])
                hi = hi - np.array([ex[1], ey[1], 0.0])
            cols = f.readline().split()[2:]
            lines = [f.readline() for _ in range(n)]
            if not lines or not lines[-1].strip():
                warnings.warn(f&amp;quot;{path}: last frame (step {step}) is truncated and was dropped&amp;quot;)
                break
            ci = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(cols)}
            for c in (&amp;quot;id&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;type&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;xu&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;yu&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;zu&amp;quot;):
                if c not in ci:
                    raise ValueError(f&amp;quot;{path}: dump lacks column '{c}' (need: id type xu yu zu)&amp;quot;)
            arr = np.array(&amp;quot; &amp;quot;.join(lines).split(), dtype=float).reshape(n, len(cols))
            order = np.argsort(arr[:, ci[&amp;quot;id&amp;quot;]])
            arr = arr[order]
            if types is None:
                types = arr[:, ci[&amp;quot;type&amp;quot;]].astype(int)
                ids0 = arr[:, ci[&amp;quot;id&amp;quot;]].astype(int)
                lo0 = lo
            elif n != len(types):
                raise ValueError(f&amp;quot;{path}: atom count changes between frames&amp;quot;)
            frames.append(arr[:, [ci[&amp;quot;xu&amp;quot;], ci[&amp;quot;yu&amp;quot;], ci[&amp;quot;zu&amp;quot;]]])
            steps.append(step)
            Ls.append(hi - lo)
    if not frames:
        raise ValueError(f&amp;quot;{path}: no frames read&amp;quot;)
    if tri_max &amp;gt;= 0:
        warnings.warn(f&amp;quot;{path}: triclinic dump header, max |tilt| = {tri_max:.4f} A: tilt IGNORED, box treated as orthogonal&amp;quot;
                      + (f&amp;quot;  [LARGE: &amp;gt; {TILT_TOL} A, distances near the box edge are off by up to this much]&amp;quot; if tri_max &amp;gt; TILT_TOL else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;))
    steps = np.array(steps)
    return Trajectory(steps=steps, time_ps=np.zeros(len(steps)), lo=lo0, L=np.array(Ls),
                      types=types, ids=ids0, pos=np.array(frames))


def prepare(cfg: dict, dump: str, tmin_ps: float | None = None, verbose: bool = True) -&amp;gt; Trajectory:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Read a dump and keep only the analysis window (t &amp;gt;= tmin_ps after MD start).

    Time zero is  last_dumped_step - run_steps  when cfg['run_steps'] is set. That also
    drops any snapshots LAMMPS wrote during `minimize` (the dump is active then).
    Without run_steps, time zero is the first frame (a warning is printed).
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    traj = read_dump(dump)
    dt = cfg[&amp;quot;dt_ps&amp;quot;]
    if cfg.get(&amp;quot;run_steps&amp;quot;):
        t0 = traj.steps[-1] - int(cfg[&amp;quot;run_steps&amp;quot;])
    else:
        t0 = traj.steps[0]
        if verbose:
            print(&amp;quot;  [note] 'run_steps' not set: assuming the first frame is the start of the MD run &amp;quot;
                  &amp;quot;(set run_steps in the config if your dump also contains minimisation frames).&amp;quot;)
    t = (traj.steps - t0) * dt
    tmin = cfg[&amp;quot;tmin_ps&amp;quot;] if tmin_ps is None else tmin_ps
    keep = (t &amp;gt;= -1e-9) &amp;amp; (t &amp;gt;= tmin - 1e-9)
    if keep.sum() &amp;lt; 10:
        raise ValueError(f&amp;quot;{dump}: only {keep.sum()} frames in the window t &amp;gt;= {tmin} ps &amp;quot;
                         f&amp;quot;(run covers {t.min():.1f}-{t.max():.1f} ps)&amp;quot;)
    n_min = int((t &amp;lt; -1e-9).sum())
    traj = Trajectory(steps=traj.steps[keep], time_ps=t[keep], lo=traj.lo, L=traj.L[keep],
                      types=traj.types, ids=traj.ids, pos=traj.pos[keep])
    if verbose:
        spacing = np.median(np.diff(traj.time_ps)) if len(traj.time_ps) &amp;gt; 1 else float(&amp;quot;nan&amp;quot;)
        print(f&amp;quot;  frames in window: {len(traj.steps)}  (t = {traj.time_ps[0]:.1f}-{traj.time_ps[-1]:.1f} ps, &amp;quot;
              f&amp;quot;spacing {spacing:.3f} ps; {n_min} pre-MD frames dropped)&amp;quot;)
    return traj


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# geometry helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def masses(cfg: dict, types: np.ndarray) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    return np.array([MASSES[cfg[&amp;quot;types&amp;quot;][int(t)]] for t in types])


def li_mask(cfg: dict, traj: Trajectory) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    return np.isin(traj.types, li_types(cfg))


def get_fold(cfg: dict, L: np.ndarray) -&amp;gt; tuple[int, int, int]:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Number of conventional cells along a, b, c.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    if cfg.get(&amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;):
        return tuple(int(x) for x in cfg[&amp;quot;fold&amp;quot;])
    f = L / cfg[&amp;quot;unit_cell_a&amp;quot;]
    fi = np.maximum(np.rint(f).astype(int), 1)
    if np.any(np.abs(f - fi) &amp;gt; 0.05 * fi):
        warnings.warn(f&amp;quot;box {L} is not an integer multiple of unit_cell_a={cfg['unit_cell_a']}; &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;using fold={tuple(fi)} - set 'fold' in the config if wrong&amp;quot;)
    return tuple(int(x) for x in fi)


def framework_drift(cfg: dict, traj: Trajectory) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Mass-weighted displacement of the non-Li COM relative to the first frame, (F,3).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    fw = ~li_mask(cfg, traj)
    m = masses(cfg, traj.types)[fw]
    com = (traj.pos[:, fw, :] * m[None, :, None]).sum(axis=1) / m.sum()
    return com - com[0]


def smooth_track(x: np.ndarray, time_ps: np.ndarray, ps: float | None) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Centred moving average (window `ps` ps, truncated at the ends) of an UNWRAPPED path x (F,3) -&amp;gt; (F,3).
    Used for the dopant position that defines the shells: removes vibration, keeps real motion (hops take longer than `ps`).
    ps &amp;lt;= 0 or a window of one frame returns x unchanged; a path that does not move is returned unchanged (up to rounding).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    if not ps or ps &amp;lt;= 0 or len(x) &amp;lt; 3:
        return x
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(time_ps)))
    w = max(1, int(round(ps / dt))) if dt &amp;gt; 0 else 1
    if w &amp;lt;= 1:
        return x
    h = w // 2
    c = np.concatenate([np.zeros((1, x.shape[1])), np.cumsum(x, axis=0)])
    i = np.arange(len(x))
    lo, hi = np.maximum(i - h, 0), np.minimum(i + h + 1, len(x))
    return (c[hi] - c[lo]) / (hi - lo)[:, None]


def dopant_xyz(cfg: dict, traj: Trajectory, drift: np.ndarray, i: int) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;(F,3) drift-removed unwrapped position of dopant atom index i, smoothed over cfg['dop_smooth_ps'] (for shells).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    return smooth_track(traj.pos[:, i, :] - drift, traj.time_ps, cfg.get(&amp;quot;dop_smooth_ps&amp;quot;))


def frac_wrapped(pos: np.ndarray, lo: np.ndarray, L: np.ndarray) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Fractional coordinates in [0,1). pos (F,N,3), L (F,3).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    return ((pos - lo) / L[:, None, :]) % 1.0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MSD (multiple time origins) and diffusive-regime check
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def msd_multi_origin(pos: np.ndarray, dt_frame_ps: float, max_lag_frac: float = 0.5, n_lags: int = 150):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;MSD(t) averaged over all atoms and all time origins in `pos` (F,N,3).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    F = pos.shape[0]
    lmax = max(2, int(F * max_lag_frac))
    lags = np.unique(np.linspace(1, lmax, n_lags).astype(int))
    msd = np.empty(len(lags))
    for i, l in enumerate(lags):
        d = pos[l:] - pos[:-l]
        msd[i] = np.mean(np.sum(d * d, axis=-1))
    return lags * dt_frame_ps, msd


def loglog_slope_warning(t_ps: np.ndarray, msd: np.ndarray, tol: float = 0.2, label: str = &amp;quot;&amp;quot;) -&amp;gt; float:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Slope of log MSD vs log t over the upper 80% of lags. WARNS ONLY - never stops the run.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    sel = t_ps &amp;gt;= t_ps[-1] * 0.2
    slope = float(np.polyfit(np.log(t_ps[sel]), np.log(msd[sel]), 1)[0])
    if abs(slope - 1.0) &amp;gt; tol:
        print(f&amp;quot;  [WARN] {label} log-log MSD slope = {slope:.2f} (diffusive regime expects ~1.0)&amp;quot;)
    return slope


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# output helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def write_dx(path: str, grid: np.ndarray, lo: np.ndarray, L: np.ndarray, title: str = &amp;quot;&amp;quot;) -&amp;gt; None:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;OpenDX scalar grid (x slowest, z fastest) at voxel centres; readable by VMD.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    nx, ny, nz = grid.shape
    d = np.asarray(L, float) / np.array([nx, ny, nz])
    origin = np.asarray(lo, float) + 0.5 * d
    with open(path, &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;) as f:
        f.write(f&amp;quot;# {title}\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write(f&amp;quot;object 1 class gridpositions counts {nx} {ny} {nz}\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write(f&amp;quot;origin {origin[0]:.6f} {origin[1]:.6f} {origin[2]:.6f}\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write(f&amp;quot;delta {d[0]:.6f} 0 0\ndelta 0 {d[1]:.6f} 0\ndelta 0 0 {d[2]:.6f}\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write(f&amp;quot;object 2 class gridconnections counts {nx} {ny} {nz}\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write(f&amp;quot;object 3 class array type double rank 0 items {grid.size} data follows\n&amp;quot;)
        flat = grid.ravel(order=&amp;quot;C&amp;quot;)
        for i in range(0, len(flat), 3):
            f.write(&amp;quot; &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{v:.6e}&amp;quot; for v in flat[i:i + 3]) + &amp;quot;\n&amp;quot;)
        f.write('attribute &amp;quot;dep&amp;quot; string &amp;quot;positions&amp;quot;\nobject &amp;quot;density&amp;quot; class field\n'
                'component &amp;quot;positions&amp;quot; value 1\ncomponent &amp;quot;connections&amp;quot; value 2\ncomponent &amp;quot;data&amp;quot; value 3\n')


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# distance shells, block-jackknife errors, hop statistics
# ONE definition used by 04, 05 and 06 so their numbers can be compared.
#   shells : edges DEFAULT_SHELLS (A) -&amp;gt; 0-3, 3-5, 5-7, &amp;gt;7 ; the last one is &amp;quot;far&amp;quot;
#   far    : 'all'  far = farther than the last edge from EVERY dopant (Li next to another dopant are dropped from far)
#            'self' far = farther than the last edge from THIS dopant only
#   ref    : one dopant label (Ga1, Ru1 ...) or 'any' = distance to the nearest dopant
#   errors : delete-one-block jackknife over DEFAULT_BLOCKS contiguous time blocks (handles ratios and differences)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DEFAULT_SHELLS = (3.0, 5.0, 7.0)
DEFAULT_BLOCKS = 5
MIN_N = 5            # fewer hops than this in a shell: SE reported as NaN
FEW_N = 20           # fewer than this: flagged '*' in the log (SE unreliable)

HOP_METHODS = {
    &amp;quot;site_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the SITE the Li leaves (fixed site list) to the dopant AT THE HOP FRAME (dopant moves; position smoothed over --dop-smooth-ps)&amp;quot;,
    &amp;quot;li_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the Li ITSELF to the dopant in the hop frame (instantaneous)&amp;quot;,
    # site-free hop detector of 06 (anchor / dwell-cluster method, no site list, no 04 output)
    &amp;quot;free_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the dwell position (anchor) the Li leaves, dopant position (smoothed) in the window of the hop [06, site-free]&amp;quot;,
    &amp;quot;free_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the Li ITSELF to the dopant in the hop frame [06, site-free]&amp;quot;,
    # O-environment hop detector of 06 (hop = the set of bonded O changes; no sites, no displacement threshold)
    &amp;quot;econ_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the dwell position the Li leaves, dopant position (smoothed) in the window of the hop, hop = change of the bonded O set [06]&amp;quot;,
    &amp;quot;econ_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell = distance of the Li ITSELF to the dopant in the hop frame, hop = change of the bonded O set [06]&amp;quot;,
}
HOP_SHORT = {&amp;quot;site_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the site the Li leaves&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;li_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the Li in the hop frame&amp;quot;,
             &amp;quot;free_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the anchor the Li leaves&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;free_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the Li in the hop frame&amp;quot;,
             &amp;quot;econ_left&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the dwell the Li leaves&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;econ_at_hop&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;shell of the Li in the hop frame&amp;quot;}


def shell_names(edges) -&amp;gt; list[str]:
    e = [0.0] + [float(x) for x in edges]
    return [f&amp;quot;{e[k]:g}-{e[k + 1]:g}A&amp;quot; for k in range(len(e) - 1)] + [f&amp;quot;&amp;gt;{e[-1]:g}A&amp;quot;]


def dist_to(li: np.ndarray, pos: np.ndarray, L: np.ndarray) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Minimum-image distance (F,N) of every Li to ONE atom. li (F,N,3), pos (F,3), L (F,3).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    d = li - pos[:, None, :]
    d -= L[:, None, :] * np.rint(d / L[:, None, :])
    return np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1)


def site_dist_moving(sites: np.ndarray, x: np.ndarray, lo: np.ndarray, L: np.ndarray, chunk: int = 2000) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;(F,S) float32 minimum-image distance of every FIXED site to ONE dopant that moves. sites (S,3) relative to the box
    origin `lo` (as in 04), x (F,3) dopant position in the dump frame (drift removed), L (3,) mean box. The site list is a
    fixed lattice; only the dopant position changes from frame to frame.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    xr = np.asarray(x, float) - lo
    out = np.empty((len(xr), len(sites)), np.float32)
    for a in range(0, len(xr), chunk):
        d = sites[None, :, :] - xr[a:a + chunk, None, :]
        d -= L * np.rint(d / L)
        out[a:a + chunk] = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1)
    return out


def shell_labels(d_this, d_near, edges, far: str = &amp;quot;all&amp;quot;) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Shell index 0..K-1 (K-1 = far) for distances d_this (any shape). d_near = distance to the NEAREST dopant (same
    shape). far='all': a point farther than the last edge from d_this's dopant but not from every dopant gets -1
    (excluded, it is neither near nor far). far='self': no exclusion.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    edges = np.asarray(edges, float)
    sh = np.searchsorted(edges, d_this)
    if far == &amp;quot;all&amp;quot;:
        sh = np.where((sh == len(edges)) &amp;amp; (np.asarray(d_near) &amp;lt;= edges[-1]), -1, sh)
    return sh


def resident_labels(sh: np.ndarray, lag: int) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;(F-lag,N) shell label of every (origin t0, Li) for lag `lag` frames, OWNERSHIP BY RESIDENCE: the label is kept only if the
    Li's shell (sh (F,N), -1 = excluded) did not change at any frame in (t0, t0+lag]; otherwise -1. A Li that leaves a shell
    and enters another is therefore counted in the new shell for the windows it spends there, never in the old one.
    Conditioning on staying biases displacements of the inner shells LOW (survivor bias): read it as 'Li that stay'.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    C = np.zeros(sh.shape, np.int32)
    C[1:] = np.cumsum(sh[1:] != sh[:-1], axis=0)
    return np.where((C[lag:] - C[:-lag]) == 0, sh[:-lag], -1)


def block_id(frames, F: int, nblocks: int) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Time block (0..nblocks-1) of frame index(es). Every script uses this same split.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    return np.minimum(np.asarray(frames) * nblocks // F, nblocks - 1)


def block_counts(labels: np.ndarray, K: int, nblocks: int, values: np.ndarray | None = None) -&amp;gt; np.ndarray:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;(nblocks,K): number of Li-frames per (time block, category), or the SUM of `values` there. labels (F,N) ints,
    -1 = ignored.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    F = labels.shape[0]
    b = block_id(np.arange(F), F, nblocks)[:, None]
    ok = labels &amp;gt;= 0
    idx = (b * K + labels)[ok]
    w = None if values is None else np.asarray(values, float)[ok]
    return np.bincount(idx, weights=w, minlength=nblocks * K).reshape(nblocks, K).astype(float)


def jackknife(fn, *blocks):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Delete-one-block jackknife. blocks: arrays (nblocks, ...) of ADDITIVE per-block sums (counts, sums of values,
    Li-time). fn(*totals) -&amp;gt; statistic (scalar or array): a mean, ratio, ratio of ratios, difference ...
    Returns (statistic on all blocks, jackknife standard error). SE is NaN unless &amp;gt;= 3 valid replicates.
    Limit: with 5 blocks the SE itself is uncertain (~35 %) and slow drifts common to all blocks are not seen.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    B = [np.asarray(b, float) for b in blocks]
    nb = B[0].shape[0]
    tot = [b.sum(axis=0) for b in B]
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter(&amp;quot;ignore&amp;quot;)
        full = np.asarray(fn(*tot), float)
        reps = np.array([fn(*[t - b[i] for t, b in zip(tot, B)]) for i in range(nb)], float)
        reps = np.where(np.isfinite(reps), reps, np.nan)
        c = np.isfinite(reps).sum(axis=0)
        dev = reps - np.nanmean(reps, axis=0)
        se = np.sqrt((c - 1) / np.maximum(c, 1) * np.nansum(dev * dev, axis=0))
    return full, np.where(c &amp;gt;= 3, se, np.nan)


def jackknife_events(values: np.ndarray, blk: np.ndarray, nblocks: int, stat=np.median):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Jackknife SE of stat(values) when the values are single events (e.g. hop dips) tagged with a time block.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    values, blk = np.asarray(values, float), np.asarray(blk)
    if len(values) == 0:
        return np.nan, np.nan
    full = float(stat(values))
    reps = np.array([stat(values[blk != b]) for b in range(nblocks) if (blk != b).any() and (blk == b).any()], float)
    if len(reps) &amp;lt; 3:
        return full, np.nan
    return full, float(np.sqrt((len(reps) - 1) / len(reps) * np.sum((reps - reps.mean()) ** 2)))


def back_flags(li, f, a, b):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Per hop (arrays in any order): eligible = the same Li hopped before; is_back = this hop undoes that previous hop
    (a-&amp;gt;b right after b-&amp;gt;a). Back-hop % is ALWAYS is_back.sum() / eligible.sum() (a Li's first hop cannot be a back-hop).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    li, f, a, b = (np.asarray(x) for x in (li, f, a, b))
    order = np.lexsort((f, li))
    l_, a_, b_ = li[order], a[order], b[order]
    same = np.r_[False, l_[1:] == l_[:-1]]
    back = same &amp;amp; np.r_[False, (a_[1:] == b_[:-1]) &amp;amp; (b_[1:] == a_[:-1])]
    elig, isb = np.empty(len(li), bool), np.empty(len(li), bool)
    elig[order], isb[order] = same, back
    return isb, elig


def shell_volume_fractions(dop_xyz: dict, L, edges, far: str = &amp;quot;all&amp;quot;, n: int = 48) -&amp;gt; dict:
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Fraction of the box volume in each shell, per reference ('any' + every dopant), on an n^3 grid (minimum image).
    Used for the 'uniform Li density' expectation. dop_xyz {label: (3,) position}, L (3,).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    L = np.asarray(L, float)
    g = [(np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n * L[i] for i in range(3)]
    P = np.stack(np.meshgrid(*g, indexing=&amp;quot;ij&amp;quot;), axis=-1).reshape(-1, 3)
    D = {}
    for lab, p in dop_xyz.items():
        d = P - np.asarray(p, float)
        d -= L * np.rint(d / L)
        D[lab] = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=1)
    dn = np.min(list(D.values()), axis=0)
    K = len(edges) + 1
    out = {}
    for lab, d in list(D.items()) + [(&amp;quot;any&amp;quot;, dn)]:
        sh = shell_labels(d, dn, edges, far)
        out[lab] = np.array([(sh == k).mean() for k in range(K)])
    return out


def hop_shell_rows(T, ref, method, names, cat, f, is_back, elig, litime_blk, nmean, F, nblocks):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Hop statistics per distance shell for ONE (reference, method). cat (n_hops,) shell of every hop (-1 = excluded),
    f hop frame, litime_blk (nblocks,K) Li-time (ps) spent in each category per time block, nmean (K,) mean Li count.
    Every number carries a jackknife SE. 'nonback' = hops that do NOT undo the previous hop of that Li.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    K = len(names)
    m = cat &amp;gt;= 0
    idx = block_id(f[m], F, nblocks) * K + cat[m]

    def cnt(w=None):
        return np.bincount(idx, weights=w, minlength=nblocks * K).reshape(nblocks, K).astype(float)

    nh, ne, nb = cnt(), cnt(elig[m].astype(float)), cnt(is_back[m].astype(float))
    t = np.asarray(litime_blk, float)
    rate, rate_se = jackknife(lambda h, tt: h / tt, nh, t)
    rr, rr_se = jackknife(lambda h, tt: (h / tt) / (h / tt)[-1], nh, t)
    bk, bk_se = jackknife(lambda b_, e_: 100 * b_ / e_, nb, ne)
    bd, bd_se = jackknife(lambda b_, e_: 100 * (b_ / e_ - (b_ / e_)[-1]), nb, ne)
    nbr, nbr_se = jackknife(lambda h, b_, tt: (h - b_) / tt, nh, nb, t)
    nr, nr_se = jackknife(lambda h, b_, tt: ((h - b_) / tt) / ((h - b_) / tt)[-1], nh, nb, t)
    # a jackknife SE from &amp;lt; MIN_N events is meaningless (0 events would give SE = 0): report NaN instead
    sh_, se_ = nh.sum(0) &amp;lt; MIN_N, ne.sum(0) &amp;lt; MIN_N
    rate_se, rr_se, nbr_se, nr_se = (np.where(sh_, np.nan, x) for x in (rate_se, rr_se, nbr_se, nr_se))
    bk_se, bd_se = (np.where(se_, np.nan, x) for x in (bk_se, bd_se))
    rows = []
    for k in range(K):
        rows.append(dict(T=T, ref=ref, method=method, shell=names[k], n_Li_mean=float(nmean[k]), n_hops=int(nh[:, k].sum()),
                         n_eligible=int(ne[:, k].sum()), n_back=int(nb[:, k].sum()), li_time_ps=float(t[:, k].sum()),
                         back_pct=bk[k], back_pct_se=bk_se[k], back_minus_far_pp=bd[k], back_minus_far_pp_se=bd_se[k],
                         rate_per_Li_ps=rate[k], rate_se=rate_se[k], rate_ratio_to_far=rr[k], rate_ratio_se=rr_se[k],
                         nonback_rate_per_Li_ps=nbr[k], nonback_rate_se=nbr_se[k], nonback_ratio_to_far=nr[k],
                         nonback_ratio_se=nr_se[k], blocks=nblocks))
    return rows


def hop_shell_analysis(T, dt, ev, names, edges, far, nblocks, state=None, site_d=None, li_d=None):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;All (reference x method) hop-shell tables for one temperature.
    ev {li,f,a,b} numpy arrays; state (F,N) committed site (-1 unknown) or None; site_d {ref: (S,) static site distance, or
    (F,S) distance of every site to the MOVING dopant in every frame (see site_dist_moving), 'any' included} or None;
    li_d {ref: (F,N) Li distance, 'any' included} or None. Method 'site_left' needs state+site_d, method 'li_at_hop' needs
    li_d. Used identically by 04 and 06.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    K = len(names)
    isb, elig = back_flags(ev[&amp;quot;li&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;a&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;b&amp;quot;])
    rows = []
    F = state.shape[0] if state is not None else next(iter(li_d.values())).shape[0]
    refs = list(site_d) if site_d else list(li_d)
    for ref in refs:
        if state is not None and site_d is not None and ref in site_d:
            ssh = shell_labels(site_d[ref], site_d[&amp;quot;any&amp;quot;], edges, far)                  # (S,) static or (F,S) moving dopant
            if ssh.ndim == 2:
                lab = np.where(state &amp;gt;= 0, ssh[np.arange(F)[:, None], np.maximum(state, 0)], -1)   # (F,N)
                cat = ssh[ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;a&amp;quot;]]                                              # shell of the vacated site at the hop frame
            else:
                lab = np.where(state &amp;gt;= 0, ssh[np.maximum(state, 0)], -1)               # (F,N)
                cat = ssh[ev[&amp;quot;a&amp;quot;]]
            bc = block_counts(lab, K, nblocks)
            rows += hop_shell_rows(T, ref, &amp;quot;site_left&amp;quot;, names, cat, ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], isb, elig, bc * dt,
                                   bc.sum(0) / F, F, nblocks)
        if li_d is not None and ref in li_d:
            lab = shell_labels(li_d[ref], li_d[&amp;quot;any&amp;quot;], edges, far)
            bc = block_counts(lab, K, nblocks)
            rows += hop_shell_rows(T, ref, &amp;quot;li_at_hop&amp;quot;, names, lab[ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], ev[&amp;quot;li&amp;quot;]], ev[&amp;quot;f&amp;quot;], isb, elig, bc * dt,
                                   bc.sum(0) / F, F, nblocks)
    return rows


def _pm(v, s, fmt):
    return (f&amp;quot;{v:{fmt}}&amp;quot; if np.isfinite(v) else &amp;quot;   nan&amp;quot;) + (f&amp;quot; +/- {s:{fmt.replace('+', '')}}&amp;quot; if np.isfinite(s) else &amp;quot; +/-  nan&amp;quot;)


def print_hop_shell(rows, T, ind=&amp;quot;  &amp;quot;):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;Log table of hop_shell_analysis rows of one temperature. Error = jackknife SE over time blocks.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    rr = [r for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] == T]
    for ref in dict.fromkeys(r[&amp;quot;ref&amp;quot;] for r in rr):
        for meth, desc in HOP_METHODS.items():
            sel = [r for r in rr if r[&amp;quot;ref&amp;quot;] == ref and r[&amp;quot;method&amp;quot;] == meth]
            if not sel:
                continue
            print(f&amp;quot;{ind}[{ref}] {desc}&amp;quot;)
            print(f&amp;quot;{ind}    shell      &amp;lt;Li&amp;gt;   hops(elig)   back-hop %          back - far (pp)      hop rate /Li/ps           &amp;quot;
                  f&amp;quot;rate / far            non-back rate / far&amp;quot;)
            for r in sel:
                far = r is sel[-1]
                star = &amp;quot;*&amp;quot; if r[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] &amp;lt; FEW_N else &amp;quot; &amp;quot;
                print(f&amp;quot;{ind}  {r['shell']:&amp;gt;8} {r['n_Li_mean']:7.2f} {r['n_hops']:6d}({r['n_eligible']:5d}){star} &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;{_pm(r['back_pct'], r['back_pct_se'], '5.1f'):&amp;gt;17}   &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;{'(reference)' if far else _pm(r['back_minus_far_pp'], r['back_minus_far_pp_se'], '+5.1f'):&amp;gt;17}   &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;{_pm(r['rate_per_Li_ps'], r['rate_se'], '.4f'):&amp;gt;21}   &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;{'1' if far else _pm(r['rate_ratio_to_far'], r['rate_ratio_se'], '.2f'):&amp;gt;17}   &amp;quot;
                      f&amp;quot;{'1' if far else _pm(r['nonback_ratio_to_far'], r['nonback_ratio_se'], '.2f'):&amp;gt;17}&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;{ind}* = fewer than {FEW_N} hops (SE unreliable; NaN below {MIN_N}).&amp;quot;)
    print(f&amp;quot;{ind}error bars = delete-one-block jackknife over {rr[0]['blocks'] if rr else '?'} time blocks (hops of one Li are &amp;quot;
          &amp;quot;correlated and the far shell is not independent of the others: treat differences &amp;lt; 2 SE as noise). &amp;quot;
          &amp;quot;back-hop % = hops that undo the Li's previous hop / hops that have a previous hop.&amp;quot;)


def plot_hop_shell(rows, names, key, ylabel, path, title, hline=None, skip_far=False, ylim=None):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;One figure, panels = reference (rows) x method (columns), x = T, one line per shell, jackknife error bars.
    ALL panels share ONE y axis so they can be compared by eye. skip_far: for ratios to far / differences from far.&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use(&amp;quot;Agg&amp;quot;)
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    refs = list(dict.fromkeys(r[&amp;quot;ref&amp;quot;] for r in rows))
    meths = [m for m in HOP_METHODS if any(r[&amp;quot;method&amp;quot;] == m for r in rows)]
    if not refs or not meths:
        return False
    K = len(names)
    cols = [plt.cm.viridis(k / max(K - 2, 1)) for k in range(K - 1)] + [&amp;quot;k&amp;quot;]
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(len(refs), len(meths), figsize=(4.8 * len(meths), 3.1 * len(refs) + 1.3), dpi=150,
                            squeeze=False, sharex=True, sharey=True)
    for ri, ref in enumerate(refs):
        for ci, meth in enumerate(meths):
            a = axs[ri][ci]
            for k in range(K - (1 if skip_far else 0)):
                sel = sorted([r for r in rows if r[&amp;quot;ref&amp;quot;] == ref and r[&amp;quot;method&amp;quot;] == meth and r[&amp;quot;shell&amp;quot;] == names[k]],
                             key=lambda r: r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;])
                if not sel:
                    continue
                a.errorbar([r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in sel], [r[key] for r in sel], [r[_se_key(key)] for r in sel], marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, ms=4,
                           capsize=2, color=cols[k], ls=&amp;quot;--&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;-&amp;quot;)
                lo_ = [r for r in sel if r[&amp;quot;n_hops&amp;quot;] &amp;lt; FEW_N]                 # too few hops: open symbol = do not trust
                if lo_:
                    a.plot([r[&amp;quot;T&amp;quot;] for r in lo_], [r[key] for r in lo_], ls=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, ms=4.5, mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, mec=cols[k], zorder=5)
            if hline is not None:
                a.axhline(hline, color=&amp;quot;0.5&amp;quot;, lw=0.8)
            nn = &amp;quot;, &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{names[k]}: {sum(r['n_hops'] for r in rows if r['ref'] == ref and r['method'] == meth and r['shell'] == names[k])}&amp;quot;
                           for k in range(K))
            a.set_title(f&amp;quot;{ref} - {meth}&amp;quot;, fontsize=10)
            a.text(0.02, 0.02, f&amp;quot;hops (all T): {nn}&amp;quot;, transform=a.transAxes, fontsize=6, color=&amp;quot;0.3&amp;quot;)
            if ri == len(refs) - 1:
                a.set_xlabel(&amp;quot;T (K)&amp;quot;)
            if ci == 0:
                a.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=8)
    if ylim:
        axs[0][0].set_ylim(*ylim)
    h = [Line2D([], [], color=cols[k], marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, lw=2, ls=&amp;quot;--&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;-&amp;quot;,
                label=f&amp;quot;{names[k]} from dopant&amp;quot; + (&amp;quot; (far, reference)&amp;quot; if k == K - 1 else &amp;quot;&amp;quot;)) for k in range(K - (1 if skip_far else 0))]
    h.append(Line2D([], [], color=&amp;quot;0.4&amp;quot;, marker=&amp;quot;o&amp;quot;, mfc=&amp;quot;white&amp;quot;, ls=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;, label=f&amp;quot;open symbol: &amp;lt; {FEW_N} hops (SE unreliable / none)&amp;quot;))
    fig.legend(handles=h, loc=&amp;quot;lower center&amp;quot;, ncol=min(len(h), 4), fontsize=8, frameon=False)
    fig.suptitle(title + &amp;quot;\n&amp;quot; + &amp;quot; | &amp;quot;.join(f&amp;quot;{m}: {HOP_SHORT[m]}&amp;quot; for m in meths) + &amp;quot; | error bars: block jackknife&amp;quot;, fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.06, 1, 0.94))
    fig.savefig(path)
    plt.close(fig)
    return True


def _se_key(key):
    return {&amp;quot;back_pct&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;back_pct_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;back_minus_far_pp&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;back_minus_far_pp_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;rate_per_Li_ps&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;rate_se&amp;quot;,
            &amp;quot;rate_ratio_to_far&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;rate_ratio_se&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;nonback_rate_per_Li_ps&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;nonback_rate_se&amp;quot;,
            &amp;quot;nonback_ratio_to_far&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;nonback_ratio_se&amp;quot;}[key]


def write_hop_shell_outputs(rows, names, outdir, name, prefix=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;):
    &amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;hop_shell_stats.csv + the shared figures (same files from 04 and 06).&amp;quot;&amp;quot;&amp;quot;
    import csv as _csv
    if not rows:
        return []
    keys = list(rows[0].keys())
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, prefix + &amp;quot;hop_shell_stats.csv&amp;quot;), &amp;quot;w&amp;quot;, newline=&amp;quot;&amp;quot;) as fh:
        w = _csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=keys)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    out = [prefix + &amp;quot;hop_shell_stats.csv&amp;quot;]
    for fn, key, yl, hl, sk, ttl in (
            (prefix + &amp;quot;back_hop_shell.png&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;back_pct&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;back-hops (% of hops with a previous hop)&amp;quot;, None, False,
             f&amp;quot;{name}: back-hop fraction by distance to the dopant&amp;quot;),
